Separating them with commas won&#8217;t help. <br><br>2. You can simply Add the choices from the provided Presets. advanced options?<\/a><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Most Demanded Free Form Fields [Previously only available in Premium version]<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Unlock in the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Image-Upload-1024x481.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can change the label and the description, and change the additional settings. Just like the Image Upload field, there are additional settings:&nbsp;<strong>Allowed File Extensions<\/strong>,&nbsp;<strong>Max File Size,<\/strong> and&nbsp;<strong>Maximum number limit on<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">You can upload all the file types that are supported by WordPress. By default, WordPress supports the following file types for uploading:\n\nImages: .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .gif\nDocuments: .pdf\nAudio: .mp3, .m4a, .ogg, .wav\nVideo: .mp4, .m4v, .mov, .wmv, .avi, .mpg, .ogv, .3gp, .3g2 <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send the uploaded file as an attachment <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span> <\/h5>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can send the uploaded file to the users in their email as an attachment. For this, go to Form Settings &gt; Email. Now, enable the option to &#8216;Send file as attachment&#8217; for the desired email notification.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2024\/09\/image-11.png\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2024\/09\/image-11-1024x470.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2915\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">You can upload the files to Google Drive and Dropbox. The Hidden Input field is not displayed on the page, so it does not allow visitors to add anything to it. The Hidden Input allows you, the webmaster, to add specific information to a form, to be passed through the form processor along with the data entered by the visitor.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Hidden-Field-1024x484.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1932\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"phone\">Phone<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Phone Number Form Field: Advanced Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/sBa7R9Wp6Gk?start=2&#038;feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Phone number field allows only adding phone numbers in the frontend.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Phone-Fields-1024x487.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1933\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Looking for more options for the Phone Field? on the contact form. autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">What You See Is What You Get, in short WYSIWYG is a system in which editing software allows content to be edited in a form that resembles its appearance when displayed as a form entry or a PDF document, etc. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/WYSIWYG-Form-Field-backend-1-1024x512.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1344\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Survey Fields<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"rating\">Rating<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Scale Rating Field: Survey Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/aHzFjk_ZNTU?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; It is a very useful field for taking reviews from users in a form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"512\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-1024x512.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1421\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-1024x512.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-300x150.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-768x384.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-360x180.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field.png 1190w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Field Options, you can change the Label, Meta Key, and Description like other form fields. There are more options specially meant for rating.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Number of Icons<\/strong>: You can enter the number for the icons to be displayed in the rating.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Rating Icon<\/strong>: This option allows you can choose a rating icon other than Star. There are five options to choose from.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Icon Color<\/strong>: Also, you can change the color of the rating icon according to your liking.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">There are few more Survey fields available in the Everest Forms-Survey, Polls, and Quiz add-on. Clicking this button, all the form field values will be cleared. You have two types of buttons on the Fields Options of this field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Reset &#8211; <\/strong>This will clear all the field values excluding the default values(in any).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Clear &#8211; <\/strong>Similar to the Reset button but will clear all the field values including the default ones.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Reset-Button-2-1024x477.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2203\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Progress<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This field will show the progress of the form as it gets filled by the user. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<div class=\"wp-block-group is-layout-flow wp-block-group-is-layout-flow\">\n<div class=\"wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-7387b849 <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Color-1-1024x448.png\" alt=\"\" the WordPress forms with no trouble. The fields will be described in the add-on section.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Payment-Fields-Updated-1-1024x486.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2435\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The payment field includes five form fields. Each of them is listed below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Single Item:<\/strong>&nbsp;This allows you to add the Name, Description, and Price for a single item on your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Multiple Choice<\/strong>:&nbsp;This allows you to add multiple items and their relative prices on your form so the users can choose one item from it. Also, there is the image choice option which lets you add an image for each item for a better representation of your items.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Checkboxes<\/strong>:&nbsp;This allows you to add multiple items and their relative prices so users can choose one or more items from it. And, you can add the images for each item like the Multiple Choice on your form.&nbsp;<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Quantity<\/strong>:&nbsp;This allows users to enter the quantity of the items selected. This allows the Total field to calculate the overall amount for the items selected X the quantity entered by the user.&nbsp;<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Subtotal: <\/strong>If you have added payment fields and assigned quantity fields to those payment fields, you can assign a subtotal field and display the subtotal for that particular payment field. <br><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/image-1-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2434\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Total:<\/strong>&nbsp;This shows the total price of all the items selected by the users.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Single Item Field: Payment Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/BccK3ye8tPs?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>Zoho<\/strong> <strong>CRM<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Install<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once the installation is finished, click on &#8216;<strong>Activate<\/strong>&#8216;. The add-on will be activated.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will need to have a Zoho <strong>Client ID and Client Secret<\/strong> in order to integrate the Everest Forms with the Zoho. Follow the steps below for this.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Getting Client ID and Client Secret<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" href=\"https:\/\/accounts.zoho.com\/developerconsole\" target=\"_blank\">Zoho API Console<\/a> and click <strong>GET STARTED<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Hover over your Server-based Application client type and click <strong>CREATE NOW<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/choose-client-api-zoho-1-2-1024x349.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1680\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Enter the&nbsp;<strong>Client Name<\/strong>,&nbsp;<strong>Homepage URL<\/strong>, and&nbsp;<strong>Authorized redirect URI<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click&nbsp;<strong>CREATE<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Create-Client-ID-Zoho-1-1024x452.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1657\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre the client ID and Secret are ready to be used.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Zoho-API-Console-1024x444.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1658\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: Please goto <a href=\"https:\/\/www.zoho.com\/\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">zoho.com<\/a>, sign in and click on CRM to integrate the Zoho CRM with the Everest Forms.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Integrating the Zoho CRM with Everest Forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have generated and copied the Client ID from the Zoho account, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Integration &gt; Zoho<\/strong>. Add the Client ID to the &#8216;<strong>Zoho Client ID<\/strong>&#8216; box. Click on &#8216;<strong>Get Access Code&#8217;.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Zoho-Integration-1024x353.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1661\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Zoho-API-Console-Client-ID-on-EVF-Settings-1024x367.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1660\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you click on the Get Access Code, the following dialog box appears. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Zoho-Connection-1024x479.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1662\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on &#8216;<strong>Accept<\/strong>&#8216;. You will get the Access Code. Copy this code and paste it into the Connection Settings. Also, add the Client Secret ID and a nickname and click on &#8216;<strong>Connect to Zoho<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Zoho-All-Cred-1024x476.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1663\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The connection is successfully created now.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/07\/Zoho-Connected-1024x414.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1664\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Adding Connections for individual forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have set up the required credentials on the main settings, it&#8217;s time for you to add a new connection for your forms. use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms > Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>Coupons<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the discount.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Status: <\/strong>Choose whether the coupon is to be activated or not. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Applicable Forms: <\/strong>You can choose on which forms that coupon can be used from this option. Select multiple by holding the &#8216;Ctrl&#8217;\/&#8217;Command&#8217; key. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Therefore, a coupon is created. For example, we have created a coupon code &#8216;Dashain2078&#8217; which gives a 10% discount on the price. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Create a Coupon Code in Bulk<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <strong>Everest Forms > Coupons.<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"466\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon-1024x466.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4618\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon-1024x466.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon-300x137.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon-768x349.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon-360x164.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/create-new-coupon.png 1345w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the <strong>Create New Coupon<\/strong> button. The form will look like:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"584\" height=\"800\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Frontend.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1553\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Frontend.png 584w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Frontend-219x300.png 219w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Frontend-360x493.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 584px) 100vw, 584px\" \/><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[48]},{"id":1531,"slug":"hubspot","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/hubspot\/","title":{"rendered":"HubSpot"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">HubSpot CRM is a free CRM platform that can be used by anyone. It comes with a robust collection of capabilities that allow a team to get a thorough picture of their sales pipeline while being time-efficient.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"installation\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest forms pro plugin and <strong>HubSpot<\/strong> add-on from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>HubSpot<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Install<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once the installation is finished, click on &#8216;<strong>Activate<\/strong>&#8216;. The add-on will be activated.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will need to have a HubSpot API in order to integrate the Everest Forms with the HubSpot. Follow the steps below for this.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setting up the API Key<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Words from HubSpot: <em>Starting November 30, 2022, all customers will no longer have access to API Keys and in-turn will no longer be able to use API Keys as an authentication method with HubSpot APIs<\/em>\nTherfore, you will have to authenticate with OAuth. \nAlso, old users can switch on or after November 30. <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Sign in to the HubSpot account, or create one from <strong><a href=\"https:\/\/app.hubspot.com\/signup-hubspot\/crm?hubs_signup-url=www.hubspot.com%2Fproducts%2Fget-started&amp;hubs_signup-cta=getstarted-growth&amp;uuid=37195cd7-dc37-4864-a870-7cabf62914fd&amp;step=landing_page\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">here.<\/a><\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Account name (on the top right) &gt; Personal &amp; Preferences &gt; Integration &gt; API<\/strong>. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"416\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/HS-API-1024x416.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1534\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/HS-API-1024x416.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/HS-API-300x122.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/HS-API-768x312.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/HS-API-360x146.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/HS-API.png 1359w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Copy the API key<\/strong> to a clipboard. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Create HubSpot List<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to your account.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>On the top right, go to Contacts &gt; Lists.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Contacts-Lists-1024x362.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2414\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on &#8216;Create List&#8217;.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Create-List-HubSpot-1024x392.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2415\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Give the name to the list.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/List-Name-and-Type-HubSpot-2-1024x376.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2416\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: While creating lists, please make sure that the list is a Static list because the HubSpot integration won't happen with the Acitve Lists.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click &#8216;Next&#8217; and then &#8216;Save List&#8217;. The list is thus created.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">HubSpot OAuth<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Log in or sign up for your <a href=\"https:\/\/app.hubspot.com\/myaccounts-beta\" data-type=\"URL\" data-id=\"https:\/\/app.hubspot.com\/myaccounts-beta\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">HubSpot Developer Account<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>First,&nbsp;<a href=\"https:\/\/developers.hubspot.com\/docs-beta\/creating-an-app\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">create an app<\/a>&nbsp;in a&nbsp;<a href=\"https:\/\/app.hubspot.com\/signup\/developers\/?_ga=2.263608837.869402458.1577115763-500942594.1573763828\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">HubSpot developer account<\/a><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/HubSport-App-Create-1-1024x459.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2240\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>After creating the app, you&#8217;ll be able to find&nbsp;the app&#8217;s client ID and client secret on the&nbsp;<em>Auth<\/em>&nbsp;page of your app settings. Copy these codes to a clipboard. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add the Redirect URL in the following format.<br><strong>yoursite.com\/wp-admin\/?evf_hubspot_auth=1<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, add the scopes given in the screenshot below. <strong>[crm.lists(Read,Write), crm.objects.contacts(Read,Write), oauth]<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Developers-HubSpot-OAuth-Scopes-1024x414.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2241\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on &#8216;Save&#8217;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, go to &#8216;<strong>Testing<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Create app test account<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/12\/image-2-1024x242.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2472\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Provide a suitable name for your app. And then follow the steps on <strong><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/hubspot\/#3-toc-title\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Create List<\/a><\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Integrating the HubSpot CRM with Everest Forms(OAuth)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have generated and copied the client ID and Secret from the HubSpot account, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Integration &gt; HubSpot<\/strong>. Add the Client ID to the &#8216;<strong>HubSpot Client ID:<\/strong>&#8216; box. Now, click on &#8216;<strong>Get Access Code&#8217;.<\/strong> <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/HubSpot-Get-Access-Code-1024x323.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2242\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>You will see the following pop-up window. They are:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Type<\/strong>: There are three parameters you can have in the popup type attribute which are popup-link,popup-button, and popup.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Text<\/strong>: You can add your custom text on this which will be the button text for the popup.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Size<\/strong>: You can set three different sizes for the popup. Or maybe you\u2019re just looking to have an awesome calculator that performs specific operations.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Everest Forms Calculations addon allows your users to perform calculations based on their input on targeted fields and display the result.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"installation\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest Forms Pro plugin and Calculations add-on from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit\u00a0<strong>Plugins -> Add New -> Upload<\/strong>\u00a0and then choose the downloaded zip file, and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms > Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>Calculation<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Install<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once the installation is finished, click on &#8216;<strong>Activate<\/strong>&#8216;. The add-on will be activated.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you\u2019ve completed the installation process, let\u2019s see how to set it up and work with it.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><strong>Enabling Calculation<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, add one or more Number fields for input. (There are more input fields that you can use. You can find them <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/calculations\/#10-toc-title\" data-type=\"link\" data-id=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/calculations\/#10-toc-title\">here<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After that, insert another Number field to display the calculation result<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to its Field Settings and toggle the Enable Calculation On for the field.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Enable-Calculation-1-1024x537.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1308\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">For example, in the above form, Number Field A and Number Field B are used for the input of the numbers and Number Field A+B is used for the output where we will have to enable the calculation. <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted has-vivid-red-color has-text-color has-link-color wp-elements-1\">Note: You can only enable calculations on the Number and Single Item field to display the results.  <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Calculation Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"450\" height=\"650\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/calculation-settings.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4158\" style=\"width:262px;height:auto\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/calculation-settings.jpg 450w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/calculation-settings-208x300.jpg 208w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/calculation-settings-354x512.jpg 354w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 450px) 100vw, 450px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Decimal Places<\/strong>: The Decimal Places field allows you to set the number of decimal values to be shown on the result. For example, suppose the result is 3.1413 and you\u2019ve selected 2 in Decimal Places. Your result will be 3.14.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Expression Field<\/strong>: This is the blank space where all the calculation expressions are to be set. It supports 26 operators and 2 parentheses \u201c()\u201d. And in this field, there are two buttons, Shortcut List on the left and &lt;> on the right.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Shortcut List:<\/strong> When you click the Shortcut List, it displays a list of operators that you can use on the Expression Field to create your formula or expression. If you select the operators from the list, it\u2019ll be inserted on the Expression Field.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Expression<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The blank field on the Calculation settings is where the expression is to be set. For adding an expression, click on the &lt;&gt; button which will show the list of calculation-ready fields. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Expression-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1311\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Available Operators<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Mathematical Operators\u00a0<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>\/: Division\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>*: Multiplication\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>+: Addition\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>-: Subtraction\u00a0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Conditional Operators<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>if(): Execute something when the condition inside the if() block is true\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>elseif(): Execute something when the condition inside the if() is false and the condition inside the elseif() is true.\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>else(): Execute something when the conditions inside the if() and elseif() are false.\u00a0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Example of Conditional Operators:&nbsp;&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">if ($FIELD_1==10):\u00a0<br>0<br>elseif($FIELD_1==5):\u00a0<br>2\u00a0<br>else:\u00a0\u00a0<br>1\u00a0<br>endif;\u00a0<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, if the input in the targeted field is 10, 0 is displayed on the result. If the input is 5, 2 is displayed. Any other input results in 1.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Mathematical Functions<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>abs(): Returns the absolute value of any given number.\u00a0\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>average(): Returns the mean or average between a set of numbers.\u00a0\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>ceil(): Rounds a number up to the nearest integer.\u00a0\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>exp(): Returns e raised to the power of the given number.\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>floor(): Rounds a number down to the nearest integer.\u00a0\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>ln(): Returns the natural logarithm of any number.\u00a0\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>log(): Return the base -10 logarithm of different numbers:<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>max(): Returns the highest value among the input arguments.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>min(): Returns the lowest value among the provided arguments.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>num(): Converts a given value to a number.\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>pi(): Returns the value of \u03c0.\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>pow(): Returns the result of raising a number to the power of another number.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>rand(): Returns a random number, usually between 0 and 1, or within a specified range.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>round(): Rounds a number to the nearest integer or to a specified number of decimal places.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>sqrt(): Returns the square root of a number.\u00a0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">String and Text Functions<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>trim(): Removes whitespace from the beginning and end of a string\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>truncate(): Returns the first length characters of the string.\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>concat(): Combines two or more strings into one string.\u00a0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">&lt;>: When you click the &lt;>, it displays available input fields that you can use in expression or formula.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">List of Fields that can be used<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Single Line Text\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Dropdown\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>First Name\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Last Name\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Number\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Email\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Multiple Choice\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Website\/URL\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Range Slider\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Payment Checkboxes\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Payment Total\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Payment Multiple Choice<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Payment Single Item\u00a0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Valid and Invalid Expressions: <\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you add an expression with correct operator signs and fields, the &#8216;<strong>Formula is valid<\/strong>&#8216; message is shown below the expression field. .<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image business is a piece of cake. Your customers can place their orders and view the total amount to be paid for.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To do so, you can use Single Item, Multiple Choice, and Checkbox payment fields.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Single Item<\/strong>: Display an individual item and its price on the form. A fixed price is to be added to the purchase.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Multiple Choice<\/strong>: Display an array of choices where only one item can be bought.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Checkbox<\/strong>: Display a list of choices where users can select multiple items at once.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Payment Example<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Let\u2019s create a form in which users can rent one of your vehicles. This will display the field where users can enter their address, and a delivery charge will be added. Apply Conditional Logic for this.\u00a0<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Number to calculate the total cost.\u00a0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">We\u2019ll use if(), else(), multiplication, and addition. Nevertheless, this might also prevent some of the site&#8217;s functionality to work as desired. <br>Due to this reason, we have made the Everest Forms plugin compatible with the AMP so that you won&#8217;t have to face any issues while wanting to make your site look and work well on mobile devices. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"requirements\">Requirements:<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Google AMP plugin. to white list domains for email?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Whitelist Email Domains?\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/pNUhupIyPRI?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Whitelisting email helps to prevent junk\/spam email addresses to submit the form. Only trusted domains can be allowed to submit the form. Whitelisting leads to improved deliverability. <br>Now, in order to enable this feature, you need to go to the email field options in your form builder.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/WhiteListed-Domain-1024x509.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1178\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, on the left, you can see the field options for the email field. Scroll down the options to find &#8216;Advanced Settings&#8217;. Now, inside this setting. you will find the section called &#8216;Whitelisted Domains&#8217;. There are two options available in the whitelisted domain section. They are:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Allowed Domains<\/strong>: When you select this option, you can enter the domain names for example, gmail.com, etc, and the users who enter these domains on the email address fields will only be able to submit the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Denied Domains<\/strong>: When you select this option, you can enter the domain names for example, gmail.com, etc, and submitting the form with these email addresses will be restricted.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When you choose Allowed Domains, the domain listed below this option will be allowed. You will have to add domains seperately for Denied Domain by choosing the Denied Domains option<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>What happens when a user tries to enter<\/strong> <strong>a<\/strong> <strong>domain name that is not allowed?<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Whenever a domain name that is not allowed is entered in the email field, the form is not submitted and throws an error just like in the image below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/WhiteListed-Domain-Error.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1183\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Block a particular Email Address<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to block a particular email address, you will need to add the following code to your theme&#8217;s functions.php file.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>add_action('everest_forms_process_validate_email', 'email_validate', 10, 3);\nfunction email_validate($field_id, $field_submit, $form_data)\n{\nif (is_array($field_submit) ) {\n$value = ! empty($field_submit&#91;'primary']) ? Sometimes similar functionality and Resources can create problems. The problem can be a design issue, or a critical issue (makes your site down). If your case is related to the designs or some features are not working on your site, you can check the plugin\/ theme conflict test. <br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"how-to-check-theme-conflict\">How To Check Theme Conflict?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">WordPress developers cannot test with every plugin, theme, and server environment, so the theme also creates conflicts, especially if your theme is custom-made or not updated by the authors. To test for a theme conflict:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Step-1:<\/strong> Please go to your Dashboard &gt;&gt; Themes.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-2:<\/strong> Activate a default theme such as Twenty Twenty-one, Twenty, Nineteen, or Seventeen.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-3:<\/strong> Check to see if the issue still exists or not.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If the issue does not exist after activating a default \u201cTwenty\u201d theme, your theme is causing a conflict with the Everest Form. If the problem continues, you should then test for plugin conflicts.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/02\/Manage-Themes-\u2039-Zakra-Book-Author-\u2014-WordPress-1024x439.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1904\" style=\"width:1160px;height:497px\" width=\"1160\" height=\"497\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"how-to-check-plugin-conflict\">How To Check Plugin Conflict?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"using-health-check-troubleshooting-plugin-recommended\">Using Health Check &amp; Troubleshooting plugin: <span class=\"recommended-button\">Recommended<\/span><\/h5>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To check conflicts on the production\/live site, we strongly&nbsp;recommend&nbsp;using&nbsp;<a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" href=\"https:\/\/wordpress.org\/plugins\/health-check\/\" target=\"_blank\">Health Check &amp; Troubleshooting<\/a>&nbsp;plugin rather than manual plugin deactivation.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Step-1:<\/strong> Please install the&nbsp;<a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" href=\"https:\/\/wordpress.org\/plugins\/health-check\/\" target=\"_blank\">Health Check &amp; Troubleshooting<\/a>&nbsp;from your Dashboard &gt;&gt; Plugins &gt;&gt; Add New.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-2:<\/strong> After Installing the Health Check plugin. You will find the troubleshoot option on each plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-3:<\/strong> Enable the Troubleshooting mode, starting with only Everest Form enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-4:<\/strong> Now Enable the other plugins one after another and check your issue on the incognito browser(private browser).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-5:<\/strong> Once you have identified the conflict, you can Disable the Troubleshoot mode. It will make your site back to normal. Report the conflict to the developers of the plugin and also to us.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<blockquote class=\"wp-block-quote is-style-default is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow\">\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> Troubleshooting Mode has&nbsp;<strong>no effect on your site visitors<\/strong>, they will continue to view your site as usual.<br> <\/p>\n<\/blockquote>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-video\"><video controls src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/02\/Plugin-Conflict-Test-Using-Health-Check-\u2011-Made-with-RecordCast-online-video-cutter.com_.mp4\"><\/video><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"using-plugin-activation-deactivation-method\">Using Plugin Activation\/ Deactivation Method<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Step-1:<\/strong> Deactivate all plugins except the Everest Form plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-2:<\/strong> Check the site to see if the issue still exists. If the problem goes away after deactivating other plugins, one of the plugins was conflicting with Everest Form. If the issue still exists, then it is not a plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-3:<\/strong> If it turns out to be a plugin conflict, you need to activate the plugins one by one until you find the plugin that causes the issue to come back.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-4:<\/strong> Once you have identified the conflict, you can report the conflict to the plugin developers and us.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<blockquote class=\"wp-block-quote is-style-default is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow\">\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> If your site is production\/live, we always recommend you keep a full backup of your site, or you can import the site on your local and staging site before doing a plugin conflict test. <br> <\/p>\n<\/blockquote>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/02\/Plugins-\u2039-userregistration-dev-\u2014-WordPress-1024x445.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1897\" style=\"width:1158px;height:503px\" width=\"1158\" height=\"503\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<div style=\"height:25px\" aria-hidden=\"true\" class=\"wp-block-spacer\"><\/div>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"critical-error-while-updaing-or-activating-the-plugin\">Critical Error- While Updaing Or Activating The Plugin.<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Sometimes installation of the plugin or updating the plugin makes your site down. In such cases, you can use your FTP or Cpanel to check which plugin is creating critical errors to the site.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Step-1:<\/strong> Login to your cPanel and go to File Manager<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-2:<\/strong> Navigate to your WordPress installation folder. Example: Public_html &gt;&gt; wp-content &gt;&gt; plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-3:<\/strong> Now rename each plugins folder to something like \u201c xyzplugin-disabled \u201d one after another.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-4:<\/strong> After renaming the specific plugin folder, Try to reload your website on your browser to check if the problem you have experienced is now gone or not.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Step-5: <\/strong>Once you have identified the conflict, please report the conflict to the respective plugin author or support.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<blockquote class=\"wp-block-quote is-style-default is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow\">\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> If you want to reactivate the plugin, please rename the plugin&#8217;s name to its original name.<br> <\/p>\n<\/blockquote>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/02\/renaming.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1898\" style=\"width:1159px;height:754px\" width=\"1159\" height=\"754\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[12]},{"id":2888,"slug":"how-to-integrate-hcaptcha","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-hcaptcha\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Integrate Hcaptcha in the Everest Forms?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Along with the Google Recaptcha, now you can integrate Hcapctha to your form to protect it from spambots\/spammers. <br>The &#8216;<strong>hCaptcha<\/strong>&#8216; is a CAPTCHA service by a company called Intuition Machines Inc which provides more reliable bot detection while being simple for humans to solve. <br>In this tutorial, we are going to give you detailed and clear-cut instructions to integrate the <strong>hCAPTCHA <\/strong>for your forms using Everest Forms for free. This helps website owners to create a form that can have similar entries for certain form fields. Users can use the URL provided by the sites to save themselves some time and decrease the confusion to fill out some fields. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Steps:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"515\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/AutoPopulate-1024x515.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1424\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/AutoPopulate-1024x515.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/AutoPopulate-300x151.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/AutoPopulate-768x386.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/AutoPopulate-360x181.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/AutoPopulate.png 1185w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Go to the required form and click on edit. <\/li><li>For the field you would like to be auto-populated, click on the field which takes you to the field settings.<\/li><li>Scroll down to the Advanced Options and click on Enable Auto populate. <\/li><li>Now, add the parameter of your choice. <\/li><li>Add the form&#8217;s shortcode to any page or post. <\/li><\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After doing the above steps, you can append the parameters to the page\/post URLs as in the example below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>http:&#47;&#47;example.com\/page-slug\/?parameter1=value1&amp;paramete2=value2..\nwhere parameter1 is the name of the parameter for the field and value1 is the required value for that field.\nhttp:\/\/evftest.test\/query-string\/?fname=wpeverest&amp;lname=test&amp;...<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, provide this URL to your users and the form will be auto-populated with the values of fields in the parameter.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2021\/12\/AutoPopulate-EVF-URL.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-965\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: On a single line text, you will be provided with an option to add an input mask.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" to Redirect Users Conditionally After Form Submission?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Conditional redirection enables you to create personalized user journeys by automatically directing form submitters to different pages based on their responses. This powerful feature transforms static forms into intelligent routing systems that enhance user experience and improve conversion rates.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Prerequisites: \n- <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/pricing\/\">Everest Forms Pro<\/a>.\n- <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/confirmations\/\">Form Confirmation Configuration<\/a>.<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After <span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">setting up the form confirmation type for form submission, you can enable\u00a0<strong>Conditional Logic<\/strong><\/span><strong>\u00a0to set up conditional confirmations<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Getting Started<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Follow these steps to get started with setting up conditional redirection after form submission:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Access Confirmation Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Navigate to <strong>Everest Forms > All Forms<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click <strong>Edit<\/strong> on your target form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Select the <strong>Settings<\/strong> tab.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Choose <strong>Confirmation<\/strong> from the sidebar menu.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Enable Conditional Logic<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Locate the <strong>&#8220;Enable Conditional Logic&#8221;<\/strong> toggle.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Switch to the <strong>ON<\/strong> position.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>The <strong>&#8220;Conditional Confirmation&#8221;<\/strong> panel will expand.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click to configure your conditional rules.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"504\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation-1024x504.png\" alt=\"Enable Conditional Logic in Form Confirmation\" class=\"wp-image-5290\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation-1024x504.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation-300x148.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation-768x378.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation-360x177.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-conditional-logic-in-form-confirmation.png 1326w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Understanding Conditional Logic on Form Confirmation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Priority System<\/strong>: When a user submits your form, Everest Forms evaluates conditional rules first. <strong>If conditions match<\/strong>, the user receives the conditional response (redirect, message, etc.). <strong>If no conditions match<\/strong>, the user automatically gets your default confirmation settings. This two-tier system ensures every submission receives appropriate feedback while enabling special experiences for targeted responses.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Configure Conditional Rule<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After enabling the <strong>conditional logic<\/strong>, you can set up the confirmations for each type. So, start by selecting the confirmation type and setting it up. Select one of three redirection options. For every redirection type, you can add various conditions. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Choose Confirmation Type<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/confirmation-types.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"704\" 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/confirmation-types-360x248.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/confirmation-types.png 1322w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Same Page<\/strong>: Users stay on the current page with a success message.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Best for: Simple confirmations and newsletter sign-ups.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use when: You want to keep users engaged on the same content.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Redirect to Custom Page<\/strong>: Users go to a specific page on your website.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Best for: Thank you pages, download pages, and next steps.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use when: You have dedicated landing pages for different user types.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Redirect to External URL<\/strong>: Users go to an external website.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Best for: Third-party services, partner sites, external resources.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use when: Integrating with external tools or services.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For more details on configuring the confirmation type, you can refer to our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/confirmations\/\">Form Confirmation<\/a>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setting Up Conditional <\/strong>For number fields, compare if the value is higher.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>less than: <\/strong>For number fields, compare if the value is lower.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 true.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>OR<\/strong>: Any one of the conditions can be true.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can click on the &#8220;-&#8221; to delete the These sub-fields will have the property of being repeated more than once while you are editing the content.<br>You can add any field from the available ones and assign them as sub-fields allowing you to create and organize complex form data with ease.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li><li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li><li>Now, you can download the Everest Forms Pro plugin and Repeater Fields addon from there.<\/li><li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li><li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li><\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Repeater Field: Advanced Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/IBfI1wBxUVY?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have completed the above installation process you will be able to use the repeater field feature for every individual form.<br>For this, go to Everest Forms > All Forms and choose the desired form where you would like to use this feature.<br>Now, edit the required form and you will be redirected form builder page. With this feature onboard, you can have all your files collected from form submissions, saved to the cloud storage directly. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Google Drive<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To set it up, follow the steps below: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To connect Google Drive with the Everest Forms, we need to follow these steps. <br><strong>1. Authenticating Google Drive<br>2. Enabling Google Drive Upload in Form Settings<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Authenticating Google Drive<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The first part is to authenticate Google Drive with your site. Browse to the Integration part of Everest Forms and click on Google Drive. <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Integration &gt; Google Drive<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Everest-Forms-Google-Drive-in-Settings-1-1024x349.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2426\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you click on the settings icon, you will see the options as shown in the screenshot below. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Everest-Forms-Google-Drive-Settings-Integration-1-1024x343.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2427\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to get the Google Client ID and Secret key?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you will need the Google Client ID and Secret key to authenticate with your Google Account. Browse to the Integration part of Everest Forms and click on Google Sheets. <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Integration &gt; Google Sheets<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/googlesheetsnew1-1024x368.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-771\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you click on the settings icon, you will see the options as shown in the screenshot below. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/01\/EVF-Google-Sheet-New-1024x352.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-976\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to get the Google Client ID and Secret key?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you will need the Google Client ID and Secret key to authenticate with your Google Account. Please follow the steps below to get these keys.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to: <a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" href=\"https:\/\/console.cloud.google.com\/\" data-type=\"URL\" data-id=\"https:\/\/console.cloud.google.com\/\" target=\"_blank\">https:\/\/console.cloud.google.com\/<\/a><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on the option \u201c<strong>Select a project<\/strong>\u201d, after which a window will pop up. Then, click on the \u201c<strong>New Project<\/strong>\u201d option. <br>Please note that if you already have a project, the project&#8217;s name will appear in the place of &#8216;<strong>Select a Project<\/strong>&#8216;. For example, in the image below, &#8216;<strong>WPEverest<\/strong>&#8216; should be clicked.<br><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"271\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Google-Sheet-Select-Project-1024x271.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3073\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Google-Sheet-Select-Project-1024x271.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Google-Sheet-Select-Project-300x79.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Google-Sheet-Select-Project-768x203.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Google-Sheet-Select-Project-360x95.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Google-Sheet-Select-Project.png 1365w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/Googe-Sheet-Step-1-1024x469.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-978\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add your project name.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Browse your location OR choose the option, \u201c<strong>No Organization<\/strong>\u201d.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>&nbsp;Click on the \u201c<strong>Create<\/strong>\u201d button.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/01\/Googe-Sheet-Step-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-981\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Navigate to the left and go to: <strong>API &amp; So if you purchased one of our products after this date then you should have used FastSpring for processing the payment. So please follow article 2(Fastspring method) else follow article 1(2checkout) method<\/span><\/em><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Update Billing Details for 2Checkout<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In order to ensure that your license renews successfully and you receive continuous updates for the plugin, you need to make sure that your payment details are correct and up to date. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Since 2checkout is our authorized payment processor, all the payment-related information like card details are stored securely by them. This means that you can easily translate the hardcoded text in the plugins without breaking anything else on your website or the plugin itself. And guess what? It is super easy!<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This tutorial will guide you stepwise step on how to do this.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To translate plugins there are many third-party plugins but for this demo, we will be using Loco Translate. Loco Translate is extremely easy to use and supports both theme and plugins.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Open your WordPress admin dashboard after logging in.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, install the<strong> Loco Translate<\/strong> plugin. For this, click on <strong>Plugins<\/strong> menu to move to the WordPress plugins page, then click on <strong>Add New<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use the search bar on the top right to search for Loco Translate and click on Install now. Once the plugin is installed, you can activate it again by pressing on Activate.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1010\" height=\"366\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_add_new_plugin-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-281\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_add_new_plugin-1.png 1010w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_add_new_plugin-1-300x109.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_add_new_plugin-1-768x278.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_add_new_plugin-1-360x130.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1010px) 100vw, 1010px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, click on Loco Translate menu on the dashboard. The Loco Translate listing should appear on the dashboard now, click on Plugins to start translating your plugins.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"663\" height=\"515\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_plugin_select-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-282\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_plugin_select-1.png 663w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_plugin_select-1-300x233.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Plugin_Translation_plugin_select-1-360x280.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 663px) 100vw, 663px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A list of plugins available will be shown to you here. <strong>languages\/plugins\/(your_plugin_name)-&lt;locale&gt;.po.<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" There are various features offered by the add-on such as <strong>limiting form entries<\/strong>, <strong>scheduling form submission dates<\/strong>, <strong>password protecting forms<\/strong>, restricting forms for users who are not logged in, and more. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> plugin and <strong>Form Restriction<\/strong> addon from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit <strong>Plugins -> Add New -> Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"mce_2\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have completed the above tasks of installing and activating the plugin, we can move on to set up this add-on for your website.<br>Go to the <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; Add New<\/strong> from your Dashboard. Here, you can either add an entirely new form or modify a current one. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the Form Builder, go to the Settings tab and you can see the <strong>Form Restriction.<\/strong> Here, you can see there are Five settings for form restriction. Each of them is explained below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2021\/11\/image-1-1024x508.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-919\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Entry Limitation <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The first setting is the Entry Limitation option. This option allows you to choose how many entries you want to receive daily, weekly, monthly, yearly or in total. To edit these settings, you need to <strong>Enable the Max number of Entries<\/strong> option. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict2.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-540\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Maximum Entries:<\/strong> There are two options available for the maximum entries. First, you need to set the number of entries you want to limit. Then, you can select the required Time Period of the entries. There are five options to choose from: Total Entries, Per Day, Per Week, Per Month and Per Year. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Max Entry Message:<\/strong> This option allows you to enter the text to be displayed when the number of entries is exceeded. Here, you can edit and enter your desired text message. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/form-sublabel-everest-forms-style-customizer.jpg\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict3.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-541\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Form Scheduling<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Form Scheduling option allows you to select a date range in which the form submissions will be allowed on your site. Here, you can choose a date when you want to start the form submissions. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, you can select a form closing date so that the users can no longer submit forms after that. The add-on provides an easy calendar option to select the dates easily. After you enable the <strong>Form Scheduling<\/strong> option, you can see the settings. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict4.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-542\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Submission Starts:<\/strong> This option allows you to select a date for the starting of form submissions. Just click on the empty field and choose the date you want to start the submissions from the calendar. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Submission Ends:<\/strong> This option allows you to select a date for the closing of the form submissions. Click on the empty field to choose the date you want to close the form submissions from the calendar. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Form Waiting Message:<\/strong> This option allows you to edit the message that will be displayed before the form submission starts. This lets the users know that the form submissions have not started yet. And, you can edit to display the date when it will start. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Form Expired Message:<\/strong> This option allows you to edit the message that will be displayed after the form submission ends. This lets the users know that the form submissions have already closed. You can change the default text as you like. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">User Restriction<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The User Restriction option allows you to restrict the form submission from the users who are not logged in. All you need to do is enable the <strong>Require user to be logged in<\/strong> option and you&#8217;re done. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Require Login Message:<\/strong> This option allows you to edit the text which is displayed if the users are not logged in already. This message alerts the users to log in first. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict5.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-543\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Password Restriction<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Password Restriction option allows you to restrict the form submissions with a Password. So, only the users with the Password will be able to submit the form. First, you need to enable the <strong>Password Protection<\/strong> option. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict6.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-544\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Password:<\/strong> This option allows you to enter any type of text as a form of password. It will restrict anyone who enters the wrong password. So, your form is password protected. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Password Form Message:<\/strong> This option allows you to edit the text which is displayed to show that the form is password protected.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>frontend<\/strong>, the Password Form Message is displayed first. Then, there is the password field. The users need to enter the <strong>correct password<\/strong> and only then will they be able to view the form itself. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict7-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-545\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Form Submission<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Form Submission option allows you to restrict the submission of the forms that are left empty by the users. After you enable the <strong>Deny empty submission<\/strong> option, your visitors won&#8217;t be able to submit an empty form on your website.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict8-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-546\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Empty Submission Message:<\/strong> This option allows you to edit the text which is displayed on top of the form after the user submits an empty form. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/form-restrict9-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-547\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Form Restriction by Country<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This option allows you to restrict the form&#8217;s submitted by the user of selected country. You can choose the country to be restricted from the Form Settings &gt; Form Restriction for the required form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2021\/11\/image-1-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-922\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Error Message: <\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2021\/11\/image-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-923\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[47]},{"id":102,"slug":"captcha","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/captcha\/","title":{"rendered":"Captcha"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Are you looking for ways to add custom Captcha on your forms?
