Well, now you can create post submission form with Everest Forms. Yes! The <strong>Post Submissions<\/strong> addon lets you integrate post and page submission from your users.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To start accepting post submissions on your forms, follow the instructions below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> plugin and <strong>Post Submissions<\/strong> addon from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit <strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"mce_2\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have completed the above tasks of installing and activating the plugin, we can move on to set up this add-on for your website.<br>Go to the <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; Add New<\/strong> from your Dashboard. Here, you can either add an entirely new form or modify a current one. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Select the one that will be most suitable for your website. Then, you can start adding the fields required for Post Submission. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After that, go to the <strong>Settings<\/strong> tab on the top and click on <strong>Post Submissions<\/strong>.  Here you need to click on the toggle button to <strong>Turn on<\/strong> Post Submission.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Post-Submission-enable-1024x442.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-457\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you enable the Post Submission Add-on in the Settings by turning on the toggle button, you can see all the post submissions fields.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Post-Submission-addon-fields.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-458\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Create a Post Submission Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, you need to create a form that includes all the post submission fields. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to the <strong>Fields<\/strong> tab in the form builder and you can see the available form fields at the left-hand side. Here, you need to create Post Submission fields for:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Post Title<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Post Content<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Post Excerpt<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Post Featured Image<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can <strong>drag and drop<\/strong> the fields from the form builder and even <strong>edit <\/strong>the label name, Descriptions and others as per your requirement<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/post-submission-form-fields-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-459\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Connect Form Fields with Post Submission<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, head over to the <strong>Settings<\/strong> tab and go to <strong>Post Submissions<\/strong>. Now, you will be able to select the fields for each option using the dropdown menu.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">So, click on the dropdown option and select the form field that you created.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Like Post Title, you can select the form field for Post Content, Post Excerpt and Post Featured Image.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/post-title-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-460\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After that, there are four more Post Submissions fields that are explained below for your convenience.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Post Type <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>Post Type<\/strong> option, you can choose whether the post submission is for WordPress <strong>Post<\/strong> or <strong>Page<\/strong>. The submitted content will either create a new post or a new page depending on which you select. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Post-Type.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-461\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Post Status<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>Post Status<\/strong> option, there are four options: Draft, Pending Review, Private and Published. So, you can choose the Post Status according to your need. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Post-Status-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-462\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Post Author<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>Post Author<\/strong> option, you can choose a specific user OR you can select the Current user. If the user filling the post submission form is already registered to your site, they will be assigned as the Post Author.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Post-Author-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-463\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Taxonomies<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Taxonomies are the way of grouping the posts in WordPress. The related and similar WordPress posts can be grouped by the Taxonomies. There are two taxonomies in WordPress: <strong>Categories<\/strong> and <strong>Tags<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This option allows you to assign the category and tag for the post submission form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/taxonomies.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-464\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Category<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Category<\/strong> option allows you to assign an existing category or allows the users to choose a category for the post submissions. Here, you have two choices. You can either assign the category for all the post submissions yourself, or you can add an option for the users to select a Category. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, you can click on the drop-down option and you can see the two options: <strong>Field value<\/strong> and <strong>Assign Category<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the <strong>Field Value<\/strong> option, you need to choose the form field that determines the post category from the user inputs. The<strong> multiple fields<\/strong>, <strong>checkbox, <\/strong>and <strong>drop-down<\/strong> fields can be used to present the categories to the form-filler.   <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This allows users to choose a category for their post submissions according to their requirements.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/post-submisssions-category-field-value.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-465\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select <strong>Assign Category<\/strong>, you need to select a category that is predetermined on your site. The categories that you have already created will appear in the drop-down list. <br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/post-submisssions-assign-category.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-466\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Tags <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Tags<\/strong> option allows you to assign existing tags or allows the users to choose tags for the post submissions.  Same as the Categories option, you have two choices. You can either assign tags for all the post submissions yourself, or you can add an option for the users to select tags.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the <strong>Field Value<\/strong> option, you need to choose the form field that determines the tags from the user inputs. The<strong> checkbox<\/strong> field can be used to present the tags to the form-filler.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This allows users to choose tags for their post submissions according to their requirements. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/post-submisssions-field-value-tag.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-467\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> When you select <strong>Assign tags<\/strong>, you need to select tags that is predetermined on your site. The tags that you have already created will appear in the drop-down list.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/post-submisssions-assign-tag.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-468\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Custom Post Meta<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Custom Post Meta<\/strong> option allows you to receive and store any type of post data that is not supported in the form already. There are limited user fields that are supported by the form such as Username, Email, Password, etc. So, with this option, you can create any field to collect user information as you require.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/custom-post-meta.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-469\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Using Custom Post Meta with ACF<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Install ACF and add a custom field<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Install and activate the Advanced Custom Fields plugin from Plugins > Add New.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/ACF-Install-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>After the installation is completed, go to Custom Fields > Add New.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Add-Custom-Fields-ACF-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>At the top, add the group name as per your desire and click on Add Field. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/image-3-1.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/image-3-1-1024x502.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2659\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will now see a dropdown menu to fill up the necessary field details. The following two details are necessary.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Field Label <\/strong>\u2013 Give your new field a name.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Field Type<\/strong> \u2013 We\u2019re going to use a simple text field, so we left this set to Text.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Field Name will automatically be assigned to the field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Custom-Fileds-Description-1.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Custom-Fileds-Description-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2660\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Save Changes<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Create a Post Submission Form and add the custom field<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Create a form on Everest Forms plugin and enable Post Submission from the Form Settings > Post Submission.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the Custom Post Meta option, add the field name from the first step and assign a field for that. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Custom-Post-Meta.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Custom-Post-Meta-1024x472.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2661\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, anything typed into the Single Line Text field will be saved in the custom field. That\u2019s it! Click Save to save your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, submit the form and the custom field will be saved on the post.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Custom-Field-on-Post.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/Custom-Field-on-Post-1024x505.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2662\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[48]},{"id":98,"slug":"user-registration","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/user-registration\/","title":{"rendered":"User Registration"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> now allows you to create users on your website. With the premium <strong>User Registration<\/strong> Feature, you can add match the form fields with the user registration fields. And, create users for your site in a few clicks.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To integrate <strong>User Registration<\/strong> on your forms, follow the instructions below: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"installation\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> plugin from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit <strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, Go to <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; Add-ons -&gt; All Features -&gt; User Registration.<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on the toggle button to <strong>Activate <\/strong>the feature.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"435\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1-1024x435.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4432\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1-1024x435.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1-768x326.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1-1536x652.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1-360x153.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/user-registration-1.png 1680w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"setup-and-configuration\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have completed the above tasks of installing and activating the plugin, we can move on to set up this addon for your website.<br>Go to the <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; Add New<\/strong> from your Dashboard. Here, you can either add an entirely new form or modify a current one.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Select the one that will be most suitable for your website. Then, you can start adding the fields required for User Registration. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After that, go to the <strong>Settings<\/strong> tab on the top and click on <strong>User Registration<\/strong>.  Here you need to click on the toggle button to <strong>Turn on<\/strong> User Registration<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon1-1-1024x440.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-754\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"integrate-user-registration-in-everest-forms\">Integrate User Registration in Everest Forms<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you enable the User Registration Add-on in the Settings by turning on the toggle button, you can see all the user registration fields. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/09\/URaddon2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-755\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"1-create-user-registration-fields\">Create User Registration Fields<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you need to insert the user registration fields on your form. So, go to the <strong>Fields<\/strong> tab, and drag and drop to add the necessary user registration fields in the form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can even edit the label name, description, and more from the <strong>Field Options<\/strong> as you like. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Make sure you insert the <strong>Email<\/strong> Field to your form otherwise you won&#8217;t be able to integrate user registration. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon3-1024x470.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-756\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"2-link-with-user-registration-fields\">Link with User Registration Fields<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After that, you need to connect these fields with User Registration <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-text-align-left wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to the <strong>Settings<\/strong> tab again and select the respective form fields using the drop down option. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon4-1024x550.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-757\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Like this, you can select the form fields of Username, First Name, Last Name, Email, Website, and others. The more important fields for User Registration are explained below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"password\">Password<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>Password<\/strong> field, you have the option to choose.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can allow users to type in a Password themselves, or <strong>Auto-generate<\/strong> a password for each user. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon5.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-758\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"user-role\">User Role<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>User Role<\/strong> field, you have the option to assign the user role directly in your form. So, you don&#8217;t need to manually assign each user roles. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can choose roles such as Subscriber, Contributor, Author, Editor, and Administrator. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon6.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-759\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"user-login-options\">User Login Options<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In <strong>User  Login Options<\/strong>, you can select the process for users to log in to your website. There are three options here: Auto Login, Admin approval, and Email confirmation. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon7.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-760\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"email-options\">Email Options<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">There are two email options for you here. The first option is to send an email to the user containing account information. And, the second one is to notify the admin via email. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon8.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-762\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"user-activation-confirmation-page\">User Activation Confirmation Page<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This option allows you to choose which page the user will be directed to when the user confirmation process is complete. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"custom-user-meta\">Custom User Meta <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Custom User Meta<\/strong> option allows you to receive and store any type of data that is not supported in the form already. There are limited user fields that are supported such as Username, Email, Password, etc. So, with this option, you can create any field to collect user information as you require. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/09\/URaddon9.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-763\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"3-add-a-user-log-in-page\">Add a User Log-in Page<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Finally, you need to create a log-in page for your users to log in to your site.  For this, <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> has provided a <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> that you can add to any page in WordPress.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong> [everest_forms_user_login] <\/strong> <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>redirect_url<\/strong> allows you to add a page URL for redirection.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>[everest_forms_user_login redirect_url=&#8221;sample-page&#8221;] <\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, you can create a new page for login and paste the shortcode there. You can also insert the page <strong>URL<\/strong> that you want the users to be directed to after login. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Login-Page-EVF-1024x481.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2009\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Your user login page will look like this in the frontend.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Login-Page-EVF-Front-1024x458.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2010\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"forgot-password-feature\">Forgot Password Feature:<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can enable the lost\/forgot password option for the login forms. For this, please go to Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; General. You can find the option to enable the forgot password.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"742\" height=\"531\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Lost-Password-EVF.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1171\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Lost-Password-EVF.png 742w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Lost-Password-EVF-300x215.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Lost-Password-EVF-360x258.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 742px) 100vw, 742px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>The result on the Login Form:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Lost-Password-EVF-Result.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1172\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add Captcha on the Login Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can integrate Google reCaptcha and hCaptcha in the registration and login form as well. You will have to add the required keys in the <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Captcha<\/strong> and can enable the Captcha support on the required forms. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">You will need to integrate Captcha with the Everest Forms. Want to know <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/captcha-settings\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">How to integrate Captcha with the Everest Forms?<\/a><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You need to add parameters to the login form&#8217;s shortcode for the Captcha. The <b>Captcha <\/b>allows you to prevent spamming on your login form.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong> [everest_forms_user_login redirect_url=&#8221;sample-page&#8221; recaptcha=&#8221;true&#8221;] <\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Learn <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/user-registration\/#3-add-a-user-log-in-page\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">How to add a Login Page for the Everest Forms?<\/a><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Captcha-support-on-EVF-User-Registration-1-1-1024x477.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2008\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Login-Page-EVF-Front-Captccha-1024x532.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2011\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> The EVF Login Form supports reCaptcha v2(I'm not a robot and invisible type), v3 and hCaptcha.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setting Up Social Login for User Registration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To enable social login and allow users to easily register and log in using their social media accounts, follow the instructions below and configure the settings accordingly.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/?post_type=docs&amp;p=4517\" data-type=\"link\" data-id=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/?post_type=docs&amp;p=4517\">How to Set Up Social Login for User Registration in Everest Forms?<\/a><\/code><\/pre>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[48]},{"id":94,"slug":"survey-polls-and-quiz","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/survey-polls-and-quiz\/","title":{"rendered":"Survey, Polls, and Quiz"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This add-on will help you to add various surveys, polls, and quizzes to your forms or websites. It can be helpful for increasing the interaction of the users. You can also know what your website users would want in your forms and website through it.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest Forms Pro plugin and Everest Forms Survey, Polls &amp; Quiz addon from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit <strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have completed the above tasks of installing and activating the plugin, we can move on to set up this addon for your website.<br>Go to the <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; Add New<\/strong> from your Dashboard. Here, you can either add an entirely new form or modify a current one.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Select the one that will be most suitable for your website. Then, you can start adding the fields for your survey, polls or quiz.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Enabling the Addon<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can enable the survey, polls, or quiz addon by going to the <strong>Settings Tab<\/strong> on your new form. Here, you can enable the <strong>Survey Reporting, Polls Results,<\/strong> and <strong>Quiz<\/strong> for your website from the <strong>Survey, Polls, and Quiz section<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/enable-the-features-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-433\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>A Survey<\/strong> is a method of examining the records with various methods. With the help of this plugin addon, the questionnaire method will be used for your survey.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Polls<\/strong> are the records for opinions or votes. A single question of a survey can also be considered as a poll with the help of this plugin addon.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Quizzes<\/strong> are games or activities where the users attempt to answer the questions or problems correctly.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Enable the features that you want to add on your website out of these.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">However, do keep in mind that <strong>you can\u2019t enable both the survey reporting and quiz at the same time<\/strong>. One must be disabled in order to enable the other. But, the Polls Results can be displayed with both Survey Reporting as well as the Quiz.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">While enabling the Quiz, you can also check the options to <strong>Shuffle the Questions<\/strong> and <strong>Enable Quiz Reporting<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Under <strong>Enable Quiz Reporting, <\/strong>you also get further more options to provide <strong>Feedback after Form Submission<\/strong>, <strong>Immediate Feedback<\/strong>, and <strong>Overall Feedback<\/strong>. All of them are available while reviewing the results.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can get expanded options to edit the feedback as well based on your score from the quiz in the <strong>Overall Feedback<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/enable-quiz-update-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz-1-1-1024x607.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-434\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Selecting the Fields<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can select different fields for the survey, polls or the quiz once you go to the <strong>Fields Tab<\/strong>. Each field is compatible differently with the Survey, Polls, or Quiz. They are mentioned as follows:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Survey<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">We are introducing three new fields for the survey in Everest Forms Survey, Polls &amp; Quiz addon. They are available in the <strong>Pro version<\/strong> only. They are:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Survey-Fields-EVF-2-1024x475.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2251\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Rating<\/strong><br>The Rating field can be used to enable your users to provide the ratings for your survey. It is shown with star ratings up to 5 stars. With the help of it, you can know how satisfied your website users are with your website or survey. You have multiple options with it as well. You can change the label, meta key, description, and number of icons. The maximum number of icons that you can display is 100. Similarly, you can also change the rating icon, icon color, and CSS classes. In addition to this, there are options for enabling conditional logic and surveys for your website.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Likert<\/strong><br>Likert is can be used for multiple-choice questions for your survey. You can edit the label, meta key, input type, number of rows and columns, and description for your survey. You also get the settings to edit the hide label checkbox, CSS classes, enable survey, and enable conditional logic for your website.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Likert Field: Survey Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/lDKH45N4fPU?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Likert Field<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul id=\"block-fa6e4be8-8843-45a7-bb70-5aecb085729a\" class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Scale Rating<\/strong><br>As the name suggests, the Scale Rating can be helpful to let your website users rate the survey questions with the help of a scale. You can update the scale from 0 to 100. The label, meta key, highest and lowest rating texts, and description can also be edited to the Scale Rating. &nbsp;You also get the settings to edit the hide label checkbox, CSS classes, enable survey, and enable conditional logic for your website.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Scale Rating Field: Survey Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/aHzFjk_ZNTU?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Yes\/No<\/strong><br>You can add yes or no questions on your survey form and with the help of this field, you can allow users to click on the thumbs up or down icon for yes or no respectively. Also, on the Advanced Settings of this field, you can change the color of the yes or no icons as well.<br>The other default fields that you can use for your website survey are <strong>Checkboxes, Multiple Choices, Dropdown, Single Line Text<\/strong>,&nbsp;and <strong>Paragraph Text<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Yes\/No Field: Survey Form Fields\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/dyg3tFTJ4j4?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Yes\/No Fields<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Polls<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The default fields that you can use for your website polls are <strong>Checkboxes, Multiple Choices,<\/strong> and <strong>Dropdown<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Quiz<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The default fields that you can use for your website quiz are <strong>Checkboxes, Multiple Choices,<\/strong> and <strong>Dropdown<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Using the Fields<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can easily add the fields by using Drag and Drop. Just drag the required field on the desired rows or column from the left side of the screen to the right side of the screen.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Survey or the Quiz is automatically enabled in the field options when you enable them in the <strong>Settings Tab<\/strong>. They can also be disabled if you want.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Additionally, the fields used for the Quiz also has extended options for <strong>Correct Answers, Feedbacks of both Correct as well as Incorrect answers, Scores,<\/strong> and <strong>Show Point with Fields<\/strong>. The scores of the questions can also be displayed at the end of it.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/quiz-options-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz-1-1024x515.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-436\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you finalize all your fields, click on Save. You will then be able to see your survey, polls, or quiz on your website now.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">However, do keep in mind that these fields will work for your website for a survey, polls or quiz only if you have purchased the Everest Forms Pro version and the Everest Form Survey, Polls, &amp; Quiz addon.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Reviewing the Results<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once your users fill out the survey, polls or the quiz, you can view the results on your dashboard as well. To view them, go to <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; Entries<\/strong>. Or, go to <strong>Everest Forms -&gt; All Forms<\/strong>. Then click on the <strong>Entries<\/strong> of your survey, polls, or the quiz.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/all-forms-entries-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-437\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can view all the detailed stats of the results entered by your users in the Everest Forms Survey, Polls, and Quiz addon. There are different ways through which they are displayed for each Survey, Polls, and Quiz.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Survey:<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Survey Report lets you view and analyze all responses collected through your Everest Forms surveys in one place. Instead of going through<br>individual entries one by one, the report gives you a visual summary of how people responded to each question in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can see how many people submitted the survey, how many completed all questions, and how each question was answered all displayed through interactive charts. The report supports multiple chart types including bar charts, pie charts, line graphs, and list views, so you can visualize your<br>data in whichever format works best for you.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"446\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352-1024x446.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5445\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352-1024x446.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352-300x131.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352-768x334.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352-1536x669.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352-360x157.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/Screenshot-2026-03-25-101352.png 1888w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Please visit the link below for a detailed guide on viewing and analyzing your survey responses with the Survey Report:\n<a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/survey-report\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener\">https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/survey-report\/<\/a><\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Polls:<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Since a single question of the survey can be regarded as a poll, the results shown for the polls are quite similar to the survey. Only the fields compatible with the Polls are shown in the results of the <strong>Entries<\/strong>. So, do make sure that you have enabled the <strong>Poll Results<\/strong> checkbox in the <strong>Settings<\/strong> and <strong>Enable Survey<\/strong> checkbox in the<strong> Field Options<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Poll-results-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-440\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The results of the poll can be viewed on either the dashboard of the website or at frontend on the website. The results of it on the Dashboard is displayed similarly with the survey with the help of various charts and other required information. It also shows the Total Votes and number of votes Skipped.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Quiz:<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The results of the quiz can also be viewed on the <strong>Entries Tab<\/strong>. You do need to remember that when you click on the Entries Tab, the last entries that you selected will be opened at the first. You need to click on the <strong>View All Summary<\/strong> and select the summaries for the quiz in order to display the quiz results on your dashboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can always go to <strong>Entries<\/strong> directly from&nbsp;<strong>All Forms<\/strong>&nbsp;on your Dashboard if you find the process very tedious.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you can view each of the answers and their scores by clicking on <strong>View<\/strong> of the respective user in the Action column.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/view-quiz-results-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-441\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you view them, the number of correct and incorrect answers along with their total scores can be seen. The <strong>Entry ID, Submitted Date, User name<\/strong>, and <strong>User IP<\/strong> are also displayed here.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Quiz-results-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-442\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The results can also be shown on the screen of the user as well in the frontend. The <strong>Total Marks<\/strong> and <strong>Marks Obtained<\/strong> are also shown along with the report of the answers entered by the users.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/quiz-results-frontend-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-443\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The options that you choose on the Settings while enabling the addon can also play a huge role in displaying the results.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you submit the <strong>Immediate feedback<\/strong> option, the feedback will appear right after you click on your answer.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/immediate-feedback-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-444\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">There are two separate options with which you can show the feedback. If you select the <strong>Feedback after form submission<\/strong>, the feedback will appear only after you submit the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Overall Feedback<\/strong> can be displayed on the immediate feedback as well as feedback after submission.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/feedback-after-form-submission-Everest-Forms-Addon-Survey-Polls-and-Quiz-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-445\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> You can easily add the feedback based on the performance of the user from the quiz. The overall feedback can be varied from the <strong>percentage of the score<\/strong> that the user obtains while taking the quiz. Just appoint the percentage of the score and add the feedback accordingly.<\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[56]},{"id":92,"slug":"multi-part-form","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/multi-part-form\/","title":{"rendered":"Multi-Part Form"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A multi part form can let you break down lengthy forms into different sections, making them less overwhelming and improving user experience.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Firstly, you need to purchase the everest forms pro plugin.<\/li><li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li><li>Now, you can download the everest forms pro plugin and Multi-Part add on from there.<\/li><li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li><li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li><\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To get started, you\u2019ll need to&nbsp;create a new form&nbsp;or edit an existing form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Navigate to Everest Forms-&gt;Select a form-&gt;Settings-&gt;Multi-Part<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/multi-part-settings-1-1024x408.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-428\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Multi-Part Form<\/strong>: Check to enable multi-part on your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Progress Indicator:<\/strong> Select theme for Part Indicator which is displayed at the top of the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Progress Indicator Color:&nbsp;<\/strong>Select the primary color for the Part Indicator theme.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Part Navigation Alignment:&nbsp;<\/strong>Select the alignment for the Next\/Previous part navigation buttons.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After enabling multi-part form from settings, you can now see the part and it&#8217;s options in the field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/multi-parts-1024x485.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-429\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From the Page Option in the left, you can assign the <strong>Part Title<\/strong>, <strong>Next Label<\/strong> and <strong>Previous Label<\/strong>. And <strong>Delete<\/strong> the part altogether. And, You can add multiple parts by clicking on <strong>Add New Part<\/strong> in the bottom right. You can also <strong>Order<\/strong> the parts by dragging the parts.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/multi-part-order-1024x478.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-430\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Frontend View:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/multi-part-frontend-1024x504.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-431\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Enable Preview<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When filling the form with multiple parts, it might be confusing to remember the details that you have entered in the previous parts. Therefore, there is a feature to enable the preview of the previous part in the Everest Forms-Multipart add-on. This feature is available for all the parts except the first one since it doesn&#8217;t have any previous parts to it. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Enable-Preview-1-1024x474.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2441\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To enable this option, select the part where you would like to show the preview. On the left-hand side, you will find the &#8216;<strong>Page Options<\/strong>&#8216;. Here, scroll down to find the &#8216;Enable Preview&#8217; option. Enable this option.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Enable-Preview-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1349\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Preview Message: <\/strong>You can write a custom HTML message on this option that will be displayed on the part where you have enabled the preview. <\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/image-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1350\" width=\"710\" height=\"135\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have set the preview message, you can now choose one of the three layouts available. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Result: <\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Preview-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1351\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Layouts<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The three layouts that are available are: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Basic Layout <\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Preview-First-Layout-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1352\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Table Layout<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Preview-Second-Layout-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1353\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Column Layout<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" Fields<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can select the desired fields from the previous parts to appear in the preview section of the current part with the help of this feature. If this option is left disabled, all the form fields prior to the current part will appear in the preview section. In the screenshot below, only three fields are selected to appear in the preview section of a part. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/image-2-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2443\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Enable Conditional Logic<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to show\/hide a particular part of the form based on the previous form fields, you can use the conditional logic feature available in the &#8216;<strong>Page Options<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/Enable-Conditional-Logic-in-Mukti-Parts-Form-1024x480.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2354\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the above example, Part Two will only be shown if the user chooses First Choice on the Multiple Choice field on Part One of the form. In this way, you can use conditional logic for making advanced forms. <\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[56]},{"id":90,"slug":"zapier","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/zapier\/","title":{"rendered":"Zapier Integration Guide"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Everest Forms and Zapier integration enables seamless connection between your WordPress forms and over 8,000 applications via Zapier&#8217;s automation platform. With our official Zapier app now published, you can create powerful automated workflows without the complexity of setting up webhooks.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Prerequisites:\n\u2705 Active Installation of Everest Forms Pro.\n\u2705 Active Zapier Account to create a Zap.<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation of the Plugin and Zapier Addon<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Before setting up the Zapier integration, you&#8217;ll need to install and configure the required components. The Zapier integration requires the Pro version of Everest Forms. If you haven&#8217;t already:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Visit the <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/\">Everest Forms website<\/a> and complete the purchase tab.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Locate <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> and click <strong>Download<\/strong> to get the plugin zip file.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In your WordPress admin dashboard, go to <strong>Plugins &gt; Add Plugin &gt; Upload Plugin<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Choose the <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> zip file you downloaded and click <strong>Install Now<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once <span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">the installation is<\/span> complete, activate the plugin using the provided license key.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong><\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a the search bar to find <strong>&#8220;Zapier&#8221;<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Locate the <strong>Zapier <\/strong>and toggle to activate.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">What Does This Integration Mean for Users?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Simplified Automation:<\/strong> Our native Zapier app provides a streamlined experience using REST API authentication, making form automation accessible to users of all technical levels<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Real-Time Data Flow:<\/strong> When someone submits your Everest Form, the data instantly flows to your connected applications\u2014whether that&#8217;s your CRM, email marketing platform, spreadsheet, or any of the 8,000+ apps available on Zapier.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Enhanced Productivity: <\/strong>Eliminate repetitive tasks and human error by automating data transfer between your forms and business applications.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup Guide on Integrating Zapier into Everest Forms (REST API <strong>Enable REST API<\/strong> option. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click <strong>Generate<\/strong> to create your API key. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Copy and save your API key securely<\/strong>. <\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/generate-rest-api-key.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" 1506w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Enable Zapier on your Everest Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 7: Set Up the Action<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Again, search for your destination app, for eg., <strong>Everest Forms. <\/strong>You can also use other apps (e.g., &#8220;Google Sheets&#8221;, &#8220;Mailchimp&#8221;, &#8220;HubSpot&#8221;) to connect Everest Forms.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Select your desired action event. For now, the plugin has only one action event &#8211; <strong>Create Entry<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"727\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event-1024x727.png\" alt=\"Choose Action Event\" class=\"wp-image-5335\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event-1024x727.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event-300x213.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event-768x545.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event-360x256.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/choose-action-event.png 1194w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">3. Connect your account for the destination app and click on <strong>Continue.<\/strong><br>4. <strong>Form<\/strong>: Connect your desired form, where you want to perform the action.<br>5. <strong>Customize Settings<\/strong>: Configure any additional options for your action. Here, you might be asked to map the data for your action form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/map-form-field-for-action-360x256.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/map-form-field-for-action.png 1232w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on <strong>Continue <\/strong>button to proceed to the next step. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/setup-and-continue-action-form-for-zapier.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"872\" height=\"878\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 8: Test and Publish<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the <strong>Test action<\/strong> to verify the integration works correctly.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Review the test results to ensure data is properly formatted.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/publish-zap.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"911\" height=\"882\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/publish-zap-768x744.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/publish-zap-360x349.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 911px) 100vw, 911px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">3. If successful, click <strong>Publish Zap<\/strong>. Your automation is now live!<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration&nbsp;(Legacy Method)<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you complete <strong>installing and activating<\/strong> both the plugin and the add-on, move on to set up the Zapier Add-on on your site.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you don&#8217;t have an account in <a href=\"https:\/\/zapier.com\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\">Zapier<\/a> already then, you need to create it before you start.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, follow the simple instructions below to integrate Zapier with Everest Forms:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Make a Zap!<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">First of all, you need to make a <strong>Zap<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Zaps<\/strong> are automated workflows that connect two or more apps. A <strong>trigger<\/strong> from one app starts the workflow, and the job gets done. A zap is completed after the required <strong>Action<\/strong> is performed.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Zapier-docs-one-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-419\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Create a Webhook<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A Zap consists of two parts:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol id=\"mce_30\" class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>When this happens<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then do this<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the &#8220;<strong>When this happens<\/strong>&#8221; section, you need to select a trigger app. Here, we choose <strong>Webhooks by Zapier<\/strong>. <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><strong>Webhooks<\/strong> are the information that is automatically passed between the connected apps when any new items are created.<\/span><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the &#8220;<strong>Then do this<\/strong>&#8221; section, you need to choose an action app where the action takes place.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, you can name your Zap in the top left bar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Zapier-Docs-two-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-420\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3: Select Catch Hook<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, you need to <strong>Choose Trigger Event<\/strong>. You need to select the Catch Hook option which allows you to pass information through the Zapier URL.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Zapier-Docs-three-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-422\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 4: Copy your generated Webhook URL<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After your <strong>Custom Webhook URL<\/strong> is created, copy it to <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" class=\"alignnone size-full wp-image-423\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/webhook-url-1.png\" alt=\"\" width=\"820\" height=\"576\"><br>Step 5: Set up Zapier in Everest Forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, navigate to <strong>Everest Forms-&gt;Select your form-&gt;Settings-&gt;Integrations-&gt;Zapier.<\/strong> First, check the<strong> Enable Zapier<\/strong> option. Then, on <strong>Webhook URL, <\/strong>paste your copied webhook.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Zapier-docs-zoom-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-424\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Use Conditional Logic<\/strong>:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Check the Use Conditional Logic option to enable <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/homepage\/conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\">Conditional Logic<\/a> and send the form data to your zap if only users fill certain fields. For example, the form data will be sent to your zap if the value of the field &#8220;Send to Zapier&#8221; is &#8220;Yes&#8221; while submitting the form.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/homepage\/how-to-use-and-or-conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\">add more than one conditions<\/a> according to your requirements.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 6: Test your Connection<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Lastly, you need to test your Hook. For this, submit a sample form entry of your WordPress form.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, go back to Zapier and click continue. Now, in the Test Your Connection section, click on <strong>Find Hook<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Zapier-Docs-five-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-425\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If the test is successful, the first step is completed.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Zapier-Test-Hook.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2189\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Connecting Web Applications with Everest Forms<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Everest Forms Zapier Add-on allows you to connect various web applications with your WordPress forms effortlessly. You can check the following articles for detailed instructions:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><a aria-label=\" (opens in a new tab)\" href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/blog\/hubspot-contact-form-wordpress\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Easily Create Contacts in HubSpot with WordPress Forms!<\/a><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><a aria-label=\" (opens in a new tab)\" href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/blog\/google-sheets-wordpress-forms\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Easily Connect Google Sheets with WordPress Forms!<\/a> <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[48]},{"id":88,"slug":"geolocation","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/geolocation\/","title":{"rendered":"Geolocation"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms geolocation addon allows you to collect and store your website visitors&#8217; geolocation data along with their form submission. Also, you can send geolocation data to the emails as well as view geolocation data from the entries page.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest forms pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest forms pro plugin and Geolocation add-on from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once, you have completed the above-mentioned task of installing and activating the plugin, now the geolocation data can be sent to the assigned admin and user email with the <code>{entry_geolocation}<\/code> smart tag.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/entry-smart-tag-1-1024x662.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-415\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will get geolocation data in your email content.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/entry-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-416\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, you can view the geolocation data from Everest forms entries page. Navigate to Everest Forms-&gt;Entries-&gt;View the entry:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/entries-location.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-417\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Address AutoComplete: <\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The users filling out the form can promptly fill the address field with the Everest Forms address autocomplete feature. This feature is easy to set up and works with Google. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setting up Geolocation Add-On for address autocomplete.<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Generating API Key<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Please follow the steps given below to enable the Google Places API and add it to the Everest Forms settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <a href=\"https:\/\/console.cloud.google.com\/apis\/dashboard\" data-type=\"URL\" data-id=\"https:\/\/console.cloud.google.com\/apis\/dashboard\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">Google Console Dashboard.<\/a><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Select a project at the top of your Google Cloud dashboard to enable the API key. (If you don&#8217;t have an existing project, you can create a project by clicking on &#8216;<strong>New Project<\/strong>&#8216;)<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Select-A-Project-Places-API-1-1024x119.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1290\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: Google will require you to enable billing on your account in order to use the Places feature.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have selected\/created a project, you will be redirected to the <strong>API &amp; Services<\/strong> dashboard of that project. If not, click on the menu icon on the top left corner of the screen and go to <strong>API &amp; Services &gt; Dashboard<\/strong>. Now, click <strong>&#8216;+Enable APIS &amp; Services&#8217;.<\/strong> <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Enable-API-And-Services-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1291\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you click on this option, it will open the API Library. From here, search and enable the following APIs:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/API-Library-Header-1-1024x241.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1292\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Places API<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Maps JavaScript API<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Places-API-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1293\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you\u2019ve enabled the required APIs, navigate to <strong>APIs &amp; Services \u00bb Credential<\/strong>s in the menu on the left. From here, click <strong>+CREATE CREDENTIALS <\/strong>and select the API key option to generate an API key.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Create-Credentials-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1294\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Your API will be presented to you in a pop-up window. Copy to clipboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/API-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1295\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Adding API Key to the Everest Forms Settings<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once the API key is generated, follow the steps below to add the key to the settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Geolocation<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add the copied API Key in <strong>Google Places API Key<\/strong> and click on Save Changes.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Adding-API-in-the-settings-1024x298.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1297\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Adding Address Autocomplete to Your Forms:<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Address-AutoComplete-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1289\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Frontend:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Type a location name and you will be shown the suggestions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"753\" height=\"414\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Address-AutoComplete.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1385\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Address-AutoComplete.jpg 753w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Address-AutoComplete-300x165.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Address-AutoComplete-360x198.jpg 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 753px) 100vw, 753px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Address Style<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Basically, there are three Styles for the address field with which you can enter the location. You can access this option from the Field Options &gt; Advanced Options. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"354\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1-1024x354.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2546\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1-1024x354.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1-300x104.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1-768x266.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1-360x125.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-1.png 1162w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">These styles are listed below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Address<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a
