class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you select <strong>Map <\/strong>as the style, you will then be shown a Map from where you can use the available pointer\/pin to select the required location. Alternatively, you can also enter the address and the Map will automatically point toward the location. Here, the additional input fields for City, State, etc won&#8217;t be shown.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/GeoLocation-Map.gif\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/GeoLocation-Map.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2549\"\/><\/a><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Frontend View For the Map Style<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Address with Map<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-4.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-4-1024x368.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2550\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This is the combination of both of the above styles. Here, you will get a map along with all the available input fields for the detailed address\/location description. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-5.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/01\/image-5.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2551\"\/><\/a><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Frontend View for Address with Map Style<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[48]},{"id":86,"slug":"stripe","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/stripe\/","title":{"rendered":"Stripe"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">This documentation covers the <strong>legacy payment<\/strong> flow using the <strong>Payments tab<\/strong>. If you're using the legacy Payments tab, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/stripe-via-payment-gateway\/\">Payment Gateway documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest form\u2019s <strong>Stripe<\/strong> addon provides you with the option of enabling Stripe as a <strong>payment gateway<\/strong> on your forms as well. Use this simple addon to provide the option for your customers to pay directly without any hassle and with complete security and assurance.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><a href=\"#new\">What&#8217;s New?<\/a><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, You have the option to set-up a&nbsp;<strong>one time charge<\/strong>&nbsp;or a&nbsp;<strong>recurring charge<\/strong>&nbsp;with this add-on. Also, the latest version of Stripe is more secure since it is <strong>SCA(Strong Customer Authentication) compatible<\/strong> now. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, you can also use <strong>Conditional Logic<\/strong> to allow users to choose between the payment gateways like PayPal and Stripe.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms <strong>v1.8.1<\/strong> brings you a <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> field under Payment Fields. It allows admins to configure subscription plans with trial periods directly from the form settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Installation<\/strong> of the Everest form stripe addon requires the following steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the <strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong> plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the Everest form pro plugin zip as well as the Stripe addon from your WPEverest account page<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest pro plugin and the Stripe addon through your <strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit <strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip files. Once the plugins have been <strong>installed<\/strong>, <strong>activate<\/strong> them and your addon should be enabled on your website.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To make sure the Stripe addon and the Payments options work perfectly on your website, we suggest you tune its settings to fit your forms better. To open the Payments settings, visit <strong>Everest Forms &#8211;&gt; Settings <\/strong>through your admin sidebar. Next, click on the <strong>Payments<\/strong> section on the top bar to open the payments settings.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Everestforms_stripe_setting-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-392\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you\u2019ve installed the addon and activated it, you will be required to activate Stripe&#8217;s features on Everest form. To proceed with the Stripe setup, you will be required to use your Stripe account\u2019s API keys. To retrieve your <strong>API keys<\/strong>, follow these simple steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Open your <strong>Stripe Dashboard.<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Visit the <strong>API keys<\/strong> page by clicking under <strong>API keys<\/strong> under the <strong>Developer<\/strong> tab on the dashboard sidebar. (<strong>Developer \u2192 API keys.<\/strong>)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Create and copy your <strong>API keys<\/strong> through this page. To learn more about the process involved in using API keys, you can refer to <a href=\"https:\/\/stripe.com\/docs\/keys\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" aria-label=\"this page (opens in a new tab)\">this page<\/a> on the Stripe website. <\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"427\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys-1024x427.png\" alt=\"Navigate to Stripe Dashboard to Get API Keys\" class=\"wp-image-4776\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys-1024x427.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys-300x125.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys-768x321.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys-1536x641.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys-360x150.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/navigate-to-stripe-dashboard-to-get-api-keys.png 1845w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><br>Next, you can change <strong>various settings<\/strong> for Stripe to make it fit better for your website.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, return to your WordPress dashboard and navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt;<\/strong> <strong>Settings &gt;<\/strong> <strong>Payments<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Under Stripe settings, you can insert the <strong>Publishable key<\/strong> and the <strong>Secret key<\/strong> to the appropriate fields. If you want to test the payment system before going live, use the Test keys that you may have received for the <strong>Stripe API.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> If you want to test the payment system before going live, click on the <strong>Enable Test mode<\/strong> option. For the test mode, you can use the Test keys that you may have received for the Stripe API. You can learn more about the Test keys and <g class=\"gr_ gr_6 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"6\" data-gr-id=\"6\">receieve<\/g> Test credit cards by referring to this <a href=\"https:\/\/stripe.com\/docs\/testing\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" aria-label=\" (opens in a new tab)\">page<\/a>.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"440\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings-1024x440.png\" alt=\"Stripe Settings\" class=\"wp-image-4779\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings-1024x440.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings-300x129.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings-768x330.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings-1536x660.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings-360x155.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/stripe-settings.png 1906w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Hit the <strong>Save Changes <\/strong>button when you complete the settings<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Stripe Form Integration<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To activate Stripe Add-on on your form, open the corresponding form through <strong>Everest forms&#8211;&gt;All Forms<\/strong> and click on the <strong>Payments<\/strong> on the form builder toolbar. Then, click on <strong>Stripe<\/strong> on the left column and simply set a checkmark on the \u2018Enable Stripe\u2019 option.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/evfstripenew1-1-1024x496.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-550\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For integrating Stripe payment onto your forms, you will be required to use the<strong> Payment fields<\/strong> section of the Everest form builder.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"536\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields-1024x536.png\" alt=\"Payment Fields\" class=\"wp-image-4777\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields-1024x536.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields-300x157.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields-768x402.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields-360x189.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/payment-fields.png 1447w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Payment Fields:<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Single item<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/single-field-1024x487-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-397\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Set the price on a <strong>single item<\/strong>. You can customize it even further by changing its features. Choose between 3 different Item types to fit your payment forms better:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Everestforms_stripe_item_type.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-398\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><g class=\"gr_ gr_3 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del\" id=\"3\" data-gr-id=\"3\"><strong>Pre defined<\/strong><\/g>: If the item type is set to pre-defined, the form users will not be able to change the price of the item on the frontend.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><g class=\"gr_ gr_8 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"8\" data-gr-id=\"8\"><strong class=\"\">User defined<\/strong><\/g>: If the item type is set to <g class=\"gr_ gr_9 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"9\" data-gr-id=\"9\"><g class=\"gr_ gr_20 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_disable_anim_appear ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"20\" data-gr-id=\"20\">user defined<\/g><\/g>, users will be allowed to set a set price for the payment straight from the frontend. This option works great for donation and charity purposes. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Hidden<\/strong>: If the item type is set to be hidden, the price will not be displayed on the frontend and the item\u2019s set price will be used as the product\u2019s price directly. <strong>U<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Multiple choice<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/multiple-field-1-1024x499.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-399\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the <strong>Multiple choice field<\/strong>, you can set various options for users to choose from. You can also set the price on each field, change their name and even change the order of the choice options by simply using drag and drop.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Checkbox<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/checkbox-2-1024x500.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-400\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>C<\/strong><g class=\"gr_ gr_14 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"14\" data-gr-id=\"14\"><strong>heckbox<\/strong><\/g><strong> field<\/strong> also exists to provide users with multiple options but unlike Multiple choices, users can choose between different items as well. This allows your users to buy multiple items at the same time. If they want to.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similar to multiple choices, you can also set\nthe name and order for each of the fields and set a flat price for all of the\nitems available.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Total<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/total-field.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-401\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Total field<\/strong> simply exists to automatically calculate the total price of all the items selected on your form. This way, you can integrate various checkboxes, multiple option fields, and single item fields and ensure that your customer doesn\u2019t lose track of the final expenses.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><strong>Credit Card<\/strong><\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you <strong>Enable Stripe<\/strong> in the Form Settings, the Payment Fields shows a new field i.e. the <strong>Credit Card<\/strong> field for stripe. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To set Stripe as the payment method for your form just drag-and-drop the <strong>Credit Card<\/strong> field to your Form. And it is that simple!<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/credit-card-field-1024x435-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-409\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong><\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field allows you to add multiple plans for your product or services so that users can choose the preferred services.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"551\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-1024x551.png\" alt=\"Subscription Plan Field\" class=\"wp-image-4780\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-1024x551.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-300x161.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-768x413.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-360x194.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field.png 1425w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From here, you can set the details like recurring, trial, and expiration dates for each plan. You can also edit the labels, pricing, and more.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To use this field, you need to first enable recurring payments for Stripe subscriptions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-recurring-payment-to-use-subscription-plan-field.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Let\u2019s see how in the section below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 id=\"new\" class=\"wp-block-heading\">What&#8217;s new?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Stripe Subscription for Recurring Payments<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms supports <strong>Recurring Payments <\/strong>if you want to charge users on a periodic basis after they submit the payment form. To use the recurring payment on your form, open the form where you want to add recurring payment. Next, click on <strong>Payments &gt; Stripe<\/strong>. Now, toggle the <strong>Enable recurring subscription payments on credit-card<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-recurring-payment-and-configure-settings.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><strong>Configure recurring payment settings:<\/strong><\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Plan Name: <\/strong>Give a name to your recurring plan.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Recurring Period:<\/strong> Select the frequency when the next transaction will be done. Also, you can select intervals between the recurring payment i.e. 15 days, 5 weeks, 6 months, and so on. But you can\u2019t make the recuring of more than 1 year.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Customer Email:<\/strong> Select the<strong> Email<\/strong> field that your user will fill in during the form submission.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">As mentioned above, Everest Forms lets you use the <strong>Subscription Plan <\/strong>field under the Payment Fields after you enable recurring payments.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">So, from <strong>Fields &gt; Payment Fields<\/strong>, drag and drop the <strong>Subscription Plan <\/strong>field into your form. The subscription plan field has 3 choices by default, which you can customize.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For more settings, click on the subscription plan field and you\u2019ll see the <strong>Field Options on the left. <\/strong>From here as well, you can reset the recurring details based on your preference. &nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also easily change the label of the plan options. To add or delete the plans, click on the <strong>Plus (+)<\/strong> and <strong>Cross(x)<\/strong> signs, respectively. Similarly, you can change pricing details by entering new pricing information into the field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-settings.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to display the form by pre-selecting one of the choices, click on the circular checkbox, like in the image below.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/pre-select-choices.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also add an image to let user glance at your product or services. To do so, enable the option <strong>Use image choices <\/strong>and add images to each plan.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/add-image-to-subscription-plan-field.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add Trial Period to Your Subscription Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also enable trial period on your plan from here. To do so, toggle the <strong>Enable Trial Period <\/strong>and set the trial period details. You can set days, weeks, months or years for trial.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-trial-period.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can add an expiration date to your subscription plan to inform users about their subscription\u2019s expiration.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Just below the<strong> Enable Trial Period<\/strong> option, you\u2019ll see <strong>Enable Expiry Date<\/strong>. Toggle it to enable the option.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/add-expire-date.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, a calendar pops up that allows you to set the expiration date.&nbsp;&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Subscription Plan Advanced Options<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The advanced options let you modify the layout of the subscription plan. You can change the layout of the subscription plan by dividing it into multiple columns or showing them in a single row (inline)<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can <strong>hide the label in<\/strong> the field. Next, adding CSS Classes and enabling\/disabling Conditional Logic is also possible in the subscription plan field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-advanced-options.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Don\u2019t forget to save the changes after you have completed the settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Lastly, the Payment Info&nbsp;of any user can be seen by the admin in the&nbsp;<strong>Entries Section<\/strong>&nbsp;of Everest Forms by clicking on the particular entry of that user.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/evfstripenew3-1-1024x531.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-552\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">User Defined Approach<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/11\/image-4-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2457\"\/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">User-Defined Approach for Stripe Subscription<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>User-Defined<\/strong> Approach &#8211; You can create a form where you can add multiple-choice or checkbox fields with various plan options to select from. Now, the users can select the desired plan and pay accordingly. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">iDeal Payment<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can integrate the iDeal payment for the Netherlands as the payment option. For this,<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to the Form builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Payment &gt; Stripe<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, enable the &#8216;Enable iDeal payment&#8217; option.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/iDeal-Payment-EVF-1-1024x511.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1360\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Save the form and you will be able to select the banks for the iDeal payment on the form(frontend).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/iDeal-Payment-Forntend-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1361\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: The currency must be selected as the 'Euro' for the iDeal payment to work.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to add Stripe as a payment option with PayPal?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you have both the <strong>Paypal standard<\/strong> and the <strong>Stripe addons<\/strong>, you can create options for your customers to choose between the two payment methods. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Firstly, insert a Multiple Choice field and add PayPal and Stripe as payment options.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Everest-Forms-Builder-\u2039-Everest-Forms-\u2014-WordPress-1-1-2-1024x443.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-410\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 id=\"mce_49\" class=\"wp-block-heading\">Compatibility with Conditional Logic <\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> allows you to <strong>enable conditional logic<\/strong> using the <strong><a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" aria-label=\"Conditional Logic (opens in a new tab)\" href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/wordpress-plugins\/everest-forms\/conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\">Conditional Logic<\/a><\/strong> addon on. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you can not only integrate conditional logic on each and every <strong>form fields<\/strong> but also, on both the <strong>payment options<\/strong>. This lets you set up a more responsive and situationally adaptive payment for your users.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you are new to Conditional Logic and want to learn what it is in detail <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/homepage\/conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Click Here<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\"> <br>Use Conditional logic for Stripe:   <\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to the <strong>Payments<\/strong> tab at the top. And make sure you enable both Paypal and Stripe. Then, Click on Stripe and <strong>Enable Conditional Logic.<\/strong> Now, make the settings so that Stripe is implemented only if the user selects the <strong>Stripe<\/strong> option in the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/enable-stripe-condition-1-2-1024x378.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-411\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, simply click on the <strong>Credit Card<\/strong> field and in the <strong>Field Options<\/strong>, if you scroll down there is the <strong>Conditional Logic<\/strong> setting. And, <strong>Enable Conditional Logic <\/strong>there.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, set the conditions so that the <strong>Credit Card<\/strong> field appears only when users choose <strong>Stripe<\/strong> as a payment option.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/credit-card-conditional-logic-1-2-1024x502.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-412\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Map extra fields with Stripe transaction details<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Map Extra fields to sync with the Stripe Payment<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can map the Stripe-supported fields of the Everest Forms when creating a subscription so that Stripe will get extra information about the users along with their email addresses. For mapping, the fields, go to the <strong>Payments &gt; Stripe<\/strong>. Enable the <strong>Credit Card <\/strong>option and now you can enable the field mapping under &#8216;<strong>Map fields to sync with Stripe<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"488\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1024x488.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4095\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1024x488.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-300x143.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-768x366.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1536x733.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-360x172.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image.png 1694w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can map the name field, description field, and phone number field. Along with these fields, you can also map the address of the user by selecting the assigned address field on the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Map Tax Number<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To map a field as a tax number field, insert a Number field on your form and label it as per your need. Then, follow the steps below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to Payments &gt; Stripe.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Enable Sync Fields with Stripe as shown in the above steps.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>On the Tax Number option, select the Number Field you have added from the dropdown.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"866\" height=\"680\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4097\" style=\"width:475px;height:auto\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1.png 866w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1-300x236.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1-768x603.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/image-1-360x283.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 866px) 100vw, 866px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Finishing up:<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once your form has been set up as you want, click on the <strong>Save button<\/strong> on the top right toolbar to save your form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>shortcode<\/strong> provided next to the Save button is the corresponding shortcode for your current form and can be used on any page or post to display your customized payment form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/wrapping-up-1-1024x431.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-413\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you&#8217;ve customized your form perfectly and inserted it onto the corresponding pages, it should be displayed on your website in a similar manner to this:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/stripe-addon-form.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-407\"\/><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[45]},{"id":83,"slug":"paypal-standard","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/paypal-standard\/","title":{"rendered":"PayPal Standard"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">This documentation covers the <strong>legacy payment<\/strong> flow using the <strong>Payments tab<\/strong>. If you're using the legacy Payments tab, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/paypal-via-payment-gateway\/\">Payment Gateway documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/paypal-standard\/\">PayPal Standard add-on<\/a> provides secure payment processing capabilities for your WordPress forms, enabling users to complete transactions through their PayPal accounts. This integration offers a trusted payment gateway that supports both one-time payments and recurring subscriptions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Prerequisites: \n- Everest Forms Pro.\n- PayPal Account for processing payments.\n<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><a href=\"#new\">What&#8217;s New in Recent Versions?<\/a><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here&#8217;s a sneak peek at our recent improvement for PayPal:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In version <strong>1.4.9<\/strong>, Everest Forms deprecated the previous <strong>Payment Field<\/strong> named <strong>Payment Options<\/strong>. Users could leverage <strong>Conditional Logic<\/strong> to dynamically select between payment gateways like PayPal and Stripe during form submission.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the release of <strong>Everest Forms v1.8.1<\/strong>, a new field &#8211; <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> is available under the <strong>Payment Field<\/strong>. It enables the admin to configure subscription plans directly through form settings. With this update, you can also add a trial period to your subscription plan.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin from WPEverest. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Access your <strong>WPEverest<\/strong> account dashboard. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Download both the main plugin and <strong>PayPal Standard <\/strong>add-on files.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go back to your <strong>WordPress dashboard &gt; Plugins &gt; Add Plugins &gt; Upload Plugin<\/strong>.<br>Now, upload the Everest Forms Pro zip file, click &#8220;<strong>Install Now<\/strong>,&#8221; and then<strong> Activate. <\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Repeat the process for the PayPal Standard addon file. Else, you can also navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong> and search for <strong>PayPal. <\/strong>Next, toggle to enable the add-on. <\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"401\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-1024x401.png\" alt=\"Enable PayPal\" class=\"wp-image-5257\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-1024x401.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-300x118.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-768x301.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-360x141.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal.png 1468w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install and activate Everest Forms Pro<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">PayPal Global Settings<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Global PayPal Settings<\/strong> allow you to configure PayPal parameters once and apply them to all forms, eliminating the need to set up PayPal credentials for each form. After you enable the plugin, you can click on the settings icon to access the settings: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"401\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings-1024x401.png\" alt=\"PayPal Settings Icon\" class=\"wp-image-5259\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings-1024x401.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings-300x118.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings-768x301.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings-360x141.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/paypal-settings.png 1468w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Else, navigate to the global PayPal configuration through your WordPress admin:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> &gt; <strong>Settings<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Select <strong>Payments<\/strong> from the settings menu.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Locate the <strong>PayPal Settings<\/strong> section.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"516\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal-1024x516.png\" alt=\"Configure PayPal\" class=\"wp-image-5258\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal-1024x516.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal-300x151.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal-768x387.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal-360x181.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/configure-paypal.png 1340w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Configure PayPal Settings through Global Payments Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can now configure the global settings for PayPal from here:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Currency<\/strong>: Set the default currency for all PayPal transactions<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Under the <strong>PayPal Settings<\/strong>, configure all the details related to your PayPal account: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>PayPal Email Address: <\/strong>Enter your primary PayPal business account email. This email will receive all payment notifications across your site and must be registered with PayPal.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Mode: <\/strong>Choose between <strong>Production and Sandbox mode<\/strong>.<br>Production mode is used for live transactions involving real money, while sandbox mode is used for testing and development purposes.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Payment Type:<\/strong> Choose between Product &amp; Service and Donation Payments. <br>Product &amp; Service is suitable for selling products, services, or digital downloads and provides a standard <strong>PayPal<\/strong> checkout experience. On the other hand, <strong>Donation Payment<\/strong> is optimized for charitable organizations and fundraising.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Cancel URL: <\/strong>Define the location to which users are redirected if they cancel the PayPal payment. So add the URL to your homepage, customer support, or anything that you feel is suitable. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on the <strong>Save Changes <\/strong>once you have completed configuring global settings for PayPal. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">PayPal Standard Settings in Individual Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To override the Global setting in the form, open the form where you want to add an individual setting. On the top, you\u2019ll see the <strong>Payments <\/strong>tab; click on it. Now, toggle the <strong>Enable PayPal Standard<\/strong>, and you\u2019ll see more settings required for PayPal:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"294\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form-1024x294.png\" alt=\"Enable PayPal in Individual Form\" class=\"wp-image-5260\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form-1024x294.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form-300x86.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form-768x220.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form-1536x441.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form-360x103.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-paypal-in-individual-form.png 1649w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From here, you can disable the Global PayPal settings for the form. Similar to the global settings, next, you can configure the PayPal: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"585\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form-1024x585.png\" alt=\"Disable Global PayPal Settings in Form\" class=\"wp-image-5261\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form-1024x585.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form-300x171.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form-768x438.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form-1536x877.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form-360x206.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/disable-global-paypal-settings-in-form.png 1545w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>PayPal Email Address:&nbsp;<\/strong>The email address of the payment receiver.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Mode:&nbsp;<\/strong>Sandbox or production mode. Sandbox mode for testing purposes.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Payment Type:<\/strong>&nbsp;Products and Services or donation. The type of payment.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Cancel URL:<\/strong> The Redirect URL if the user cancels after redirecting to PayPal.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Conditional Logic:<\/strong> If you also use the <a href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/wordpress-plugins\/everest-forms\/conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\">Conditional Logic<\/a> add-on, you can use it to provide a payment option between the PayPal and Stripe add-ons.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Find all the documentation related to <strong>Conditional Logic <\/strong>from <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/conditional-logic\/\">here<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can enable a recurring subscription. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Subscriptions<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To charge users periodically, you can enable recurring payments on the registration form, and then the users will be regularly charged.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To do so, enable <strong>Recurring Subscription on PayPal.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"420\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription-1024x420.png\" alt=\"enable Recurring Subscription\" class=\"wp-image-5262\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription-1024x420.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription-300x123.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription-768x315.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription-1536x630.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription-360x148.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/enable-recurring-subscription.png 1854w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Plan Name:<\/strong> Give your recurring plan a name. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Customer Email<\/strong>: Select the Email field that your user will fill in during the form submission.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Get more information on Subscription Plans from <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/paypal-standard\/#8-toc-title\">here<\/a>. <strong>Save<\/strong> the changes after you\u2019re done.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">More on Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Let\u2019s proceed with setting up the payment fields on your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Payment Fields<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you haven&#8217;t added any payment-related fields to your form, you&#8217;ll get multiple fields under the <strong>Payment Fields<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Single Item<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Start by dragging a single item and the total payment field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/PayMent-Fields-1-1024x513.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1749\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Single item Settings:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/resend-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-382\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Item Price: <\/strong>Set the default price to the item<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>item type<\/strong> description:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Pre-Defined<\/strong>: If set predefined, users won\u2019t be allowed to change the item price later in the frontend.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>User Defined<\/strong>: If set user-defined, users can change the item price from the frontend ( suitable for donation type ).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Hidden<\/strong>: If the single item input type is hidden, It will not be displayed on the front and the price set on the item price will be the product\u2019s price.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Multiple Choice<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the multiple items fields, you can provide options for the user to select the option. You can set each item&#8217;s price as shown in the figure below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/multiple-items-1-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-383\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Quantity<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This field can be assigned to a single item or multiple-choice field and it maps the quantity entered by the users.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Quantity-Field-1024x505.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1750\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Checkbox<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the checkbox field, you can allow the users to select two or more options. The item&#8217;s price can be set as below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PayPal-Checkbox-1024x444.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2351\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Total<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><b>The total<\/b> field helps you to display the total of all payment fields&#8217; prices.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Frontend View:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/05\/frontend-display-payments.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-384\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Subscription Plan on Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field allows you to add multiple plans for your product or services so that users can choose the preferred services.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"551\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-1024x551.png\" alt=\"Subscription Plan Field\" class=\"wp-image-4780\" 1425w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can add the Subscription Plan field to your form only when you enable recurring payment on your form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-recurring-payment-to-use-subscription-plan-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"542\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-recurring-payment-to-use-subscription-plan-field-1024x542.png\" alt=\"Enable Recurring Payment to Use Subscription Plan Field\" class=\"wp-image-4773\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-recurring-payment-to-use-subscription-plan-field-1024x542.png 1024w, \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To do this, <strong>enable recurring subscription payments on PayPal<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, drag and drop the <strong>Subscription Plan <\/strong>field into your form. The subscription plan field has three choices by default.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"587\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field-1024x587.png\" alt=\"Drag-and-drop Subscription Plan Field\" class=\"wp-image-4771\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field-1024x587.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field-300x172.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field-768x440.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field-360x206.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/drag-and-drop-subscription-plan-field.png 1414w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can edit them by clicking on the field. As you click on the subscription plan field, you will see the Field <strong>Options tab<\/strong> on the left side with all the required settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also easily change the label of the plan options. To add or delete the plans, click on the <strong>Plus (+)<\/strong> and <strong>Cross(x)<\/strong> signs, respectively. Similarly, you can change pricing details by entering new pricing information into the field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Recurring Details<\/strong>: Select the frequency at which the next transaction will be done. Also, you can select intervals between the recurring payment, i.e., 15 days, 5 weeks, 6 months, and so on.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-settings.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to display the form by pre-selecting one of the choices, click on the circular checkbox, like in the image below.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/pre-select-choices.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also add an image to let user glance at your product or services. To do so, enable the option <strong>Use image choices <\/strong>and add images to each plan.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/add-image-to-subscription-plan-field.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a Trial Period to Your Subscription Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also enable trial period on your plan from here. To do so, toggle the <strong>Enable Trial Period <\/strong>and set the trial period details. You can set days, weeks, months or years for trial.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-trial-period.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can add an expiration date to your subscription plan to inform users about their subscription\u2019s expiration.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">You&#8217;ll see Enable Expiry Date below the<strong>&nbsp;Enable Trial Period<\/strong>&nbsp;option<\/span>. Toggle it to enable the option.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/add-expire-date.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, a calendar pops up that allows you to set the expiration date.&nbsp;&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Subscription Plan Advanced Options<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The advanced options let you modify the layout of the subscription plan. You can change the design of the subscription plan by dividing it into multiple columns or showing them in a single row (inline)<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can <strong>hide the label in<\/strong> the field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, adding CSS Classes and enabling\/disabling Conditional Logic is also possible in the subscription plan field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/subscription-plan-field-advanced-options.png\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Don\u2019t forget to save the changes after you have completed the settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to add PayPal as a payment method with Stripe?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you have both the <strong>Paypal standard<\/strong> and the <strong>Stripe addons<\/strong>, you can create options for your customers to choose between the two payment methods.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Firstly, insert a Multiple Choice field and add PayPal and Stripe as payment options.<br><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" class=\"alignnone size-full wp-image-387\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Everest-Forms-Builder-\u2039-Everest-Forms-\u2014-WordPress-1.png\" alt=\"\" width=\"1197\" height=\"518\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Compatibility with Conditional Logic:<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> allows you to <strong>enable conditional logic<\/strong> using the <strong><a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" aria-label=\"Conditional Logic (opens in a new tab)\" href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/wordpress-plugins\/everest-forms\/conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\">Conditional Logic<\/a><\/strong> addon on. You can not only integrate conditional logic on each and every <strong>form field<\/strong> but also, on both the <strong>payment options<\/strong>. This lets you set up a more responsive and situationally adaptive payment for your users.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you are new to Conditional Logic and want to learn what it is in detail <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/homepage\/conditional-logic\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Click Here<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Use Conditional logic for PayPal:<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to the <strong>Payments<\/strong> tab at the top. And make sure you enable both Paypal and Stripe. Then, Click on PayPal and <strong>Enable Conditional Logic.<\/strong> Now, make the settings so that Stripe is implemented only if the user selects the <strong>PayPal<\/strong> option in the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/paypal-conditional-logic-1-1024x486.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-388\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once the payment is done successfully, user will receive the confirmation similar to the image below: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/07\/Paypal-Subscription-Payment-Done-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2478\"\/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">PayPal Notification after the payment is completed<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[45]},{"id":81,"slug":"pdf-form-submission","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/pdf-form-submission\/","title":{"rendered":"PDF Form Submission"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"pdf-form-submission-updated\">PDF Form Submission<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This add-on helps you to send the form-filled data as a PDF file as an email attachment. You can send this attachment to both admin and the selected user. Also, admins at any time can export the PDF file of any selected entries.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"installation\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest Forms pro plugin and PDF form submission add-on from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"setup-and-configuration\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once, you have completed the above-mentioned task of installing and activating the plugin, now the PDF file can be attached to the emails sent to the admin and user.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Global Settings<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/New-PDF-Submission-Settings-1-395x1024.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1718\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>PDF Template: <\/strong>You can now determine which template&#8217;s format to be used while sending the pdf. The Left Header Template is the default one.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Header Logo: <\/strong>If you have any specific logo designs, you can add them over here.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>PDF Header Details:<\/strong> Options whether to include form name, site title, or site URL in the PDF file. You can select multiple options with CTRL + Click.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Custom Header Text: <\/strong>Set your own custom header texts for the PDFs.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Page Size: <\/strong>You can select the type of the page (example: A4, Letter, Legal, Ledger, Executive ) in which you want to generate the PDF.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Orientation: <\/strong>Options to select the orientation of the page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Font: <\/strong>Choose between multiple font types for the PDF text.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Font Size: <\/strong>Type the font size of the PDF text.(example: 20)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Design-related options: <\/strong>Font Color, Background Color, Header Font Color, Header Background Color.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Enable Background Image<\/strong>: Enable this option to set a background image on the PDF file.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Background Image<\/strong>: Select an image for the background.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Background Image Opacity<\/strong>: Select the opacity intensity of the background image. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Enable Multi Column PDF<\/strong>: Two-column pdf is generated from the two-column form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Include Field Description:<\/strong> Field description can be included on the PDF file.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Download PDF after form submit:<\/strong> If you enable this option, PDF gets downloaded automatically when a user submits the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Reverse Text:<\/strong> Choose &#8220;Yes&#8221; if you want to display the text in reverse in PDF i.e. from Right to Left. (Usually selected in case of Arabic and Hebrew Script)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Hide Empty Fields: <\/strong>Enabling this option lets you hide the empty fields in your PDFs.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">The PDF file that is generated will support the Chinese Characters as well. <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h6 class=\"wp-block-heading\">PDF Header Details<\/h6>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/07\/image-5.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"673\" height=\"425\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/07\/image-5.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2802\" style=\"width:385px;height:243px\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/07\/image-5.png 673w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/07\/image-5-300x189.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/07\/image-5-360x227.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 673px) 100vw, 673px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: You will need to include items from the available options in the PDF Header Details if you want to show these items in the PDF Header. For example, upload a logo and then select Header Logo in the PDF Header Details<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Form Settings<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/docspdf-3-1-1-1024x476.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-377\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Attach Form Data in PDF Format:<\/strong> Check to send PDF file as an email attachment.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Show Header In Attachment PDF file:<\/strong> Check to show headers selected in global settings such as site title, form title, etc.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"exporting-pdf-file\">Exporting PDF File<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also at any time export the PDF file of the entries from the entries section. Navigate to Everest Forms-&gt; <strong>Entries<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/docspdf-4-1-1024x473.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-378\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"how-to-send-pdf-files-to-users\">How to send PDF files to users? <\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">By default, the PDF files are sent to the admin only. However, you can send the PDF files to the user that fills the form. For this, please follow the instruction below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add an email field on your form.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Email-Field-PDF-1-1024x418.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1265\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to the <strong>Form Settings &gt; Email<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Add New Email<\/strong>&#8216; on the left side.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-1024x516.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1266\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Enter the desired name and click on &#8216;OK&#8217;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, on the &#8216;To Address&#8217; box, select &#8216;Email&#8217; from the dropdown. (Email is the label of the email field)<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/User-PDF-Add-Email-1-1024x512.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1267\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Scroll down and enable &#8216;Attach form data in PDF format.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Add-New-Email-2-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1268\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Please note that, on the &#8216;To Address&#8217; option, you can add as many email addresses as you like. Each email address must be followed by a comma.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send PDF Download URL in the email<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Sometimes the size of the PDF generated can be heavy to be sent to the email as an attachment. In this case, you can add <strong>{pdf_link}<\/strong> smart tag on the email message section to send PDF Download URL. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"540\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PDF-Link-in-Email-1024x540.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2298\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PDF-Link-in-Email-1024x540.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PDF-Link-in-Email-300x158.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PDF-Link-in-Email-768x405.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PDF-Link-in-Email-360x190.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/PDF-Link-in-Email.png 1188w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Individual Form&#8217;s PDF Setting:<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want a different PDF as per your forms, you can have different settings for different forms. On the form editor, go to <strong>Form Settings &gt; PDF Submission<\/strong> for the respective form. Check the &#8216;<strong>PDF Submission<\/strong>&#8216; and set the PDF as per your requirements. You can browse the image below to check the available settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/PDF-Setting-Main-2-452x1024.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1990\" style=\"width:518px;height:1174px\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the individual form&#8217;s PDF settings, you will see an option at the bottom named &#8216;<strong>Enable Choose Fields<\/strong>&#8216;. Enable this option to choose the fields that have to appear in the PDF file for that respective form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/PDF-Enable-Choose-Fields-2-1024x511.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1991\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: Once you enable the 'PDF Submission' for any individual form, it will override the Global PDF Settings for that respective form.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to use the Custom PDF name?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to use the custom PDF name, Go to Everest Forms > Form Settings > PDF Submission<strong>,<\/strong> scroll down a bit, and you will see the <strong>Custom PDF Name <\/strong>option. Set your condition and hit Save.<\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[4]},{"id":71,"slug":"additional-premium-fields","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/additional-premium-fields\/","title":{"rendered":"Additional Premium Fields"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Extend the functionality of your forms with Everest Forms pro.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"advanced-fields\"><strong>Advanced Fields<\/strong><\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"image-upload\">Image Upload<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Insert the Image Upload field to let your form fillers to upload any image (jpg, png, gif,&nbsp; etc.) type in the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2022\/02\/Image-Upload-1024x410.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1055\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Additional Settings:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Allowed File Extensions:&nbsp;<\/strong>You can restrict to only allow certain types of images, such as gif, png, BMP, etc. Enter the multiple extensions separated by comma (,).<\/li><li><strong>Max File Size<\/strong>: Enter the max file size, in megabytes, to allow. If left blank, the value defaults to the maximum size the server allows.<\/li><li><strong>Maximum number limit on uploads<\/strong>: Allows you to enter the number of image uploads for this field.<\/li><li><strong>Store image in WordPress Media Library<\/strong>:&nbsp;Check this option to store the final uploaded file in the WordPress Media Library.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/Image-Upload1-1024x363.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1057\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"file-upload\">File Upload<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similar to image upload, you can use the File Upload field to allow the form fillers to upload any type of file on the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/File-Upload-1-1024x362.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1059\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can change the label and the description, and change the additional settings. Just like the Image Upload field, there are additional settings:&nbsp;<strong>Allowed File Extensions<\/strong>,&nbsp;<strong>Max File Size,<\/strong> and&nbsp;<strong>Maximum number limit on uploads<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/File-upload1-1-1024x364.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1062\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> With Everest Forms Pro v1.3.7 and above, you can also save the uploaded files directly to <strong>Google Drive and Dropbox<\/strong>. Learn more about it <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/how-to-upload-files-to-google-drive-or-dropbox\/\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><strong>here<\/strong><\/a>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To upload files with extensions that are not supported by the WordPress, please visit the <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/how-to-allow-files-with-extensions-that-arent-supported-by-the-wordpress-default-to-be-uploaded\/\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">Link <\/a> for more details.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"hidden-field\">Hidden Field<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can drag and drop the hidden field. The Hidden Input field is not displayed on the page, so it does not allow visitors to add anything to it. The Hidden Input allows you, the webmaster, to add specific information to a form, to be passed through the form processor along with the data entered by the visitor.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"366\" id=\"phone\">Phone<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Phone number field allows to only add phone number in the frontend.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"367\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone-1024x367.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1177\"
