settings, go to the Field Options by clicking on the Phone Field. Here, you can change the&nbsp;<em>Label<\/em>,&nbsp;<em>Meta Key<\/em>,&nbsp;<em>Description<\/em>&nbsp;like any other field. But, there are two main field options for Phone: Format and Input Mask<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the&nbsp;<strong>Format<\/strong>&nbsp;as&nbsp;<em>Default<\/em>, you can see the&nbsp;<strong>Input Mask<\/strong>&nbsp;option too where you can enter a custom input mask.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone1-1024x391.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1179\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the&nbsp;<strong>Format<\/strong>&nbsp;as&nbsp;<em>Smart<\/em>, it displays the country flag with validation and you don\u2019t need to enter an Input Mask.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone2-1024x374.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1180\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone3.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1181\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"password\">Password<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A&nbsp;<strong>password field<\/strong>&nbsp;on your form doesn\u2019t display what the user types. Each keystroke is represented on the screen by a placeholder character, such as an asterisk or a bullet so that someone looking over the user\u2019s shoulder can\u2019t see what they type.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Password-Field-1-1024x370.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1606\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To change the Password settings, go to the Field Options. Here, you\u2019ll see various options such as label, description, and required. Other than that there are three options for the Password field.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Password Confirmation:<\/strong>&nbsp;Enabling this option will add a Confirm Password field to your Password Field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh3.googleusercontent.com\/AjsZb09U1_UsvPwWkcgasIo-rHnGvrS3QCBhm7K5_vRKFv0S4L-v283gbPgsPQf87CnP_doh9PjNo9h0a0sBLZ3Iq0dbkz0sWIc-KlfdA26Yg4Xt9rI3OyyDiz5afeGgnKxm82ck\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Password Strength Meter:<\/strong>&nbsp;Enabling this option will show a password strength meter on the frontend of the form to the user input. There are two choices: Simple Text and Progress Bar.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh5.googleusercontent.com\/9FjuElMdzugkjvC63hWQJ7qQ88yJR23KMaqo3r5hijUG5QqUgs6H2N5ci9speWZuLBy0yVN8kpkLCn8PwdmPMuLIbovQafv06seDBKx5EZResApSKcoQ0fqJpBBKwxLzEWpsL7te\" class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"address\">Address<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Address Field allows users to enter all address related information. Address field includes:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Address Line 1<\/strong><\/li><li><strong>Address Line 2<\/strong><\/li><li><strong>City<\/strong><\/li><li><strong>State\/Province\/Region<\/strong><\/li><li><strong>Zip\/Postal Code<\/strong><\/li><li><strong>Country<\/strong><\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh4.googleusercontent.com\/G9lMksaxiowCMihQXRAewmJTiMfmGB49QE2AdzrVgjGyc7XxXNAAZtTaNUY7H_X5wJ77nmMtAI74iUuBfYac7gngZMUIU7vobh8AwYm9I68pwj9B8rtib5KjqTEpN8iqUUXB_RAj\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the Field Option, you have the regular settings for label, description and required. Also, when you check the required option, you can edit the required message for each subfield of the Address field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh5.googleusercontent.com\/ePRw6uAAUPaDewz7IJ6RL2nLJxoM5gXcJPWn6PPy6wVlcSEnNU44Grsf4Lo9Bl5aYxBThna-6DG5C7Ojqal0Nk7SsRS6A1dJVzGLBwQ4T4pZMDOajvI-Ov_navoX5Zo8Dpvpxxwa\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Advanced Options, you can see the edit option for each subfield. To change the sub-labels, click on the edition option or simply click on the subfield name.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh5.googleusercontent.com\/4-FJphIg9YccWMLYxEaq4yrFJiBXvzRnxR7mxRGCKBktGqRNhAleu9opOU63H4W_ftrycn5NNhoElZZIFGILFoK2ikcYZbNBMOi5j-nc5_xOQjTzfbyA9dDKd5KG5qpYFFXCUQps\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You also have the option to show only certain address fields from settings. By default all address fields are displayed, if you want to remove certain fields, check the checkbox indicated in the above figure.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh6.googleusercontent.com\/vM4yNY3SKk5A7_kjOJGlP56TblFOTQFu8QxRxdZylVOWV6xIcUog2q3HkhfFyVOz_5ssajaox2KTO6GvPHh4Mv8PtER41dgWHZVuayJom2DyL_Zv1yHLkkfGdn98k0I-gF_VOszW\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"country\">Country<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the country fields, you can list all countries in a select box for the user to choose from.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"800\" height=\"513\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Country-Listing.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1415\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Country-Listing.png 800w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Country-Listing-300x192.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Country-Listing-768x492.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Country-Listing-360x231.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 800px) 100vw, 800px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Field Options, you can see the settings for the Country field such as label, description, required, and more. The main settings are:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Country Flags:<\/strong>&nbsp;This option allows you to show the country flags on the frontend of the form.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Default Country:<\/strong>&nbsp;This option allows you to choose the default country so, the users cannot choose the country.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh3.googleusercontent.com\/dlXVZH22ERJ5EhL4RJal9aqNBgMIzMmu2z4hW7an_oEyQwU8geiPlcYorzE2O6bkQEPP9Up44qf1w28RmVcvXGyCtEHYQwFXZzlNPVb_8gU_AKAVWz54zFMY6brKU2Y8H_bcwgAr\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"signature\">Signature<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Signature field allows users to add E Signatures on the WordPress forms with no trouble. Form fillers can use the Touch Screens or even Mouse to sign on the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"512\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Signature-Field-1024x512.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1417\" class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"range-slider\">Range Slider<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Range Slider Field<\/strong> allows you to display a <strong>beautiful user-friendly<\/strong> bar slider at the front end of the form where users can select a value from the provided data by dragging the <strong>Handle<\/strong>.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider1-1024x501.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-568\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On clicking on this field, you will see General Option to set the <strong>Minimum Value<\/strong> and <strong>Maximum value<\/strong> of the Slider. You will also be able to <strong>set the Step<\/strong> that the <strong>Sliding Handle<\/strong> will take when <strong>sliding in in action<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-569\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on the <strong>Advanced Options<\/strong> and then you will see the <strong>Advanced Settings<\/strong> for the Range Slider. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider3.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-570\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider4.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-571\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Advanced Options, you are provided a lot of options to customize with. I will briefly let you know the purpose of each of them.<br><br><strong>Skin:<\/strong> This option can be used to select the <strong>Sliding Handle Type<\/strong>. You have these options to choose from. Flat, Big, Modern. Round, Sharp Square.<br><br><strong>Slider Handle Color:<\/strong> This option lets you select the color for the slider handle.<br><br><strong>Slider Highlight Color: <\/strong>This option lets you select the color for the dragged slider track. This color will be shown in the track from the starting point to the current position of the slider handle. <br><br><strong>Slider Track Color:<\/strong> This option lets you select the color of the entire track from Starting to Ending on which the handle will slide.  <br><br><strong>For example,<\/strong> the Slider will look like this if I selected Red Handle color, Green Highlight color, and Blue Track color for the slider. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider6.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-573\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, Let&#8217;s discuss other options: <br><br><strong>Show Grid For The Slider:<\/strong> If you enable this option, you will be shown a grid of your Minimum and Maximum Value in the front end.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider7-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-574\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Show Slider Prefix Postfix<\/strong>: After you enable this option. you will be able to show the<strong> Prefix and Postfix<\/strong> <strong>on top of Starting and Ending points<\/strong> of the Slider. In the screenshot below, I have used<strong> Go and Stop<\/strong> as <strong>Prefix and Postfix<\/strong> for the Slider. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/rangeslider8-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-575\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Show Slider Input:<\/strong> Enabling or Disabling this option will let you show\/hide the input box at the end of the slider. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"privacy-policy\">Privacy Policy<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This field allows you to place a \u201cterms of use\u201d or &#8220;site policy&#8221; on the contact form. You can type the Consent Message, link pages of your site, and <strong>external URL<\/strong> in the Consent Message.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/privacy1-1-1024x434.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-578\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you click on this field, you will see the available options for playing around with this field in the field options. You will see three major sections, the <strong>Consent Message<\/strong>, <strong>Add a local Privacy Policy Page<\/strong>, and <strong>Add a custom URL<\/strong> option. We will discuss these one by one.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Consent Message:<\/strong> The Consent Message is the input section where you can enter the message that the form visitors can read directly. This where you can write &#8220;<strong>I accept the Terms and Conditions<\/strong>&#8220;, &#8220;<strong>I agree to the site&#8217;s User Policy<\/strong>&#8221; or anything that suits your need. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/privacy4-1-1024x347.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-579\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you enter the Consent Message and save the form, preview it, you will see the Consent Message on the front-end as it is. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/privacy5-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-580\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Add a Local Privacy Policy Page:<\/strong> This option lets you to <strong>add a link<\/strong> to one or more pages of your website in the Consent Message. You can simply <strong>select a page from the dropdown<\/strong> and then <strong>click on<\/strong> <strong>Add Page<\/strong>. It will <strong>add a syntax<\/strong> in your consent message which <strong>creates a hyperlink<\/strong>. You will be able to see the changes instantly in the form builder about how it will look in the frontend. For example: see screenshot. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/privacy6-1-1024x367.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-581\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> <em>The best part about adding a local page is, the user will be able to see the link in the frontend and when they will click on the link, the contents of the link will be shown them as a dropdown instantly. Thus, form visitors will not be redirected to a different page during the form submission process if the link is a local link. See the example below.<\/em><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/privacy7-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-582\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the screenshot above, you can see, the contents of the Sample Page is shown when I click on the link. Similarly it goes away when you click on the link again. Thus is will be a beneficial addition to your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Add a Custom URL:<\/strong> This option lets you <strong>add a custom URL<\/strong> in the consent message which could be <strong>an external link<\/strong> to a page containing the Privacy Policy. You will need to <strong>type the label name<\/strong> for the URL and then <strong>enter the URL<\/strong> in the respective input boxes. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/07\/privacy8-1-1024x417.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-583\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong><em> In this case, if the user clicks on the link, the link will open in a new tab so that the user can read the terms there and then agree or disagree with the form submission.<\/em><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">WYSIWYG<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">What You See Is What You Get, in short WYSIWYG is a system in which editing software allows content to be edited in a form that resembles its appearance when displayed as a form entry or a PDF document, etc. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/WYSIWYG-Form-Field-backend-1-1024x512.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1344\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On clicking on this field, you will see Field Options, where you can set different attributes for the WYSIWYG form field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Field-Options-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1345\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Here, 'Default Value' can be set by clicking on the &lt;&gt; icon. The value includes, site admin email, name, URL, page title, etc.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"payment-fields\">Payment Fields<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Payments Fields are helpful if you have the Payments add-on such as&nbsp;<strong>PayPal Standard<\/strong>&nbsp;activated. The fields will be described in the add-on section.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh5.googleusercontent.com\/rrl7nRve2fBWTflfHq2p63TIc6NOkycSxUd9X7a8-Dw3yzh_luP3QZOg_EWZQeuLCxFZZB967vhcK0gdF_mHOU2-cjbS0BXiguTfVggo9ggILb2QnMC1bR_VoPvmz9kwxHwC4YKN\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The payment field includes five form fields. Each of them is listed below:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Single Item:<\/strong>&nbsp;Allows you to add the Name, Description, and Price for a single item on your form.<\/li><li><strong>Multiple Choice:<\/strong>&nbsp;This allows you to add multiple items and their relative prices on your form so the users can choose one item from it. Also, there is the image choice option which lets you add an image for each item for a better representation of your items.<\/li><li><strong>Checkboxes:<\/strong>&nbsp;This allows you to add multiple items and their relative prices so users can choose one or more items from it. And, you can add the images for each item like the Multiple Choice on your form.&nbsp;<\/li><li><strong>Quantity:<\/strong>&nbsp;This allows users to enter the quantity of the items selected. This allows the Total field to calculate the overall amount for the items selected X the quantity entered by the user.&nbsp;<\/li><li><strong>Total:<\/strong>&nbsp;This shows the total price of all the items selected by the users.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"survey-fields\">Survey Fields<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"rating\">Rating&nbsp;<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Everest Forms pro version, you can use the&nbsp;<strong>Rating<\/strong>&nbsp;field which comes under the Survey Fields. It is a very useful field for taking reviews from the users in a form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"512\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-1024x512.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1421\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-1024x512.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-300x150.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-768x384.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-360x180.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field.png 1190w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Field Options, you can change the Label, Meta Key, and Description like other form fields. There are more options specially meant for rating.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Number of Icons<\/strong>: You can enter the number for the icons to be displayed in the rating.<\/li><li><strong>Rating Icon<\/strong>: This option allows you can choose a rating icon other than Star. There are five options to choose from.<\/li><li><strong>Icon Color<\/strong>: Also, you can change the color of the rating icon according to your liking.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"412\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-Settings-1024x412.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1422\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-Settings-1024x412.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-Settings-300x121.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-Settings-768x309.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-Settings-360x145.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/Rating-Field-Settings.png 1185w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[4]},{"id":65,"slug":"emails-are-not-being-delivered","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/emails-are-not-being-delivered\/","title":{"rendered":"Emails are not being delivered?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"4 Reasons Why Your Contact Form is Not Sending Emails\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/8MQDegFGVJk?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you are unable to receive email notifications triggered by the plugin then there could be several reasons for this. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"email-configuration\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#email-configuration\"><h3 class=\"inline\">Email Settings Configuration Issue<\/h3>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you are not able to receive the email when a form is submitted, then the first thing you need to check is whether your email settings <g class=\"gr_ gr_5 gr-alert gr_gramm gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim Grammar multiReplace\" id=\"5\" data-gr-id=\"5\">is<\/g> configured correctly or not. For this go to your form settings-&gt;Email-&gt;Admin Email Notifications and check if the To Address, From Address, Reply To Address, From Name are correct. Check the link below for more detail<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/homepage\/email-settings\/#admin-email-notification\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">Setting up Admin Email Notification<\/a><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, we have seen few users using same email address for To Address, Reply To Address. This also stops your email from being delivered.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Email Blocked by Spam Filters<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Sometimes, your emails can get blocked due to some blacklisted or promotional words in your&nbsp;<strong>Email Subject<\/strong>. We have seen this issue in a few hosting environments. These types of emails are blocked not by the email service providers but, by the website\u2019s Hosting Servers.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you don\u2019t receive the email, then your emails are most probably blocked due to some spammy-looking content in your Email Subject.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can change the email subject to something simpler like \u201cNew Form Entry\u201d and check if you can receive the emails.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Please check your email and, try to avoid spammy-looking words in the subject to prevent your emails from getting blocked.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Emails going to Spam Folder<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Sometimes your email might move to the spam folder. While there are many reasons why an email is marked as spam and would be a different topic to discuss. You might be looking at your Inbox while your email might have been received in your spam box. So make sure that your emails triggered by the plugin are in the spam box.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Server Configuration Issues<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the context of email delivery, the key server configuration is the PHP mail function. This function allows PHP scripts (like your WordPress site) to send emails. If it&#8217;s disabled, emails won&#8217;t be sent. Additionally, if you&#8217;re using an SMTP plugin, its settings (like server address, port, username, and password) must be correct for successful email delivery. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Testing Email Delivery Logs for Server Issues<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To find out if the issue is with the server, you can always check the Email Delivery logs via third-party plugins like Check &amp; Log Email. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Install and activate the <a href=\"https:\/\/wordpress.org\/plugins\/check-email\/\" data-type=\"link\" data-id=\"https:\/\/wordpress.org\/plugins\/check-email\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Check &amp; Log Email plugin<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Enable the email delivery log from this plugin&#8217;s settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Check &amp; Log Email &gt; Email Logs<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpuserregistration.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/08\/Email-Logs.png\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpuserregistration.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/08\/Email-Logs-1024x354.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5072\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">As shown in the above image, the Green Check indicates that the emails were sent from the plugin. However, if the emails weren&#8217;t delivered to the respective receivers, this means the issue is with the email server. In this case, you can contact the email server and report the issue with the delivery.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[17,12]},{"id":63,"slug":"how-to-send-email-to-different-recipients-using-conditional-logic","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-send-email-to-different-recipients-using-conditional-logic\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Send Email to Different Recipients Using Conditional Logic?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Send Email via Conditional Logic\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/ZG4VdIEQyXU?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Want to allow the users to choose different email recipients in your form? No worries! Everest Forms Conditional Logic has got you covered! The Pro version of the plugin allows you to implement conditional logic to each form field. And, you can also implement it to send email notifications to multiple recipients according to user choice.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this tutorial, we will show you the easy steps to use the conditional logic to send email notifications to multiple recipients. So, Let\u2019s get started!<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Select a Form or Create a New Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">First, you need to go to the form you want to integrate multiple email recipients. If you have not created a form yet you can create a new one. <br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/WordPress-Popup-Form-Everest-Forms-Templates-1024x497-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-328\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Add Field for User Choice<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, you need to add a choice field so that the users can choose an option. And, according to this user input, the Email Notification will be sent to different emails. So, in the Everest Forms form builder, you can find various choice fields to add options such as Multiple Choice, Checkboxes, etc.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Add-Multiple-Choice-.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-329\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, we are using the Multiple Choice field. After you insert the Multiple Choice field to your form, go to the Field Options and you can see the various settings for the field. Here, you can change the Label, Description and enable Required option. Also, you can change the text of the options and add image options too. You can add as many options as you like and delete them according to your needs.<br><br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Multiple-choice-Everest-Forms.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-330\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> Make sure you enable the <strong>Required<\/strong> option on the Field Options as this field is needed to determine the emails. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Required.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-331\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3: Add Multiple Email Notifications<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you add the option field to your WordPress form, you can now work on the Email Notification for that form. So, go to the <strong>Settings<\/strong> tab on the top-left of the form builder. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, go to the <strong>Email<\/strong> Settings and you can see the Default Email Notification settings. To add a new email, you need to click on the <strong>Add New Email<\/strong> button on the left-hand side. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Add-Email.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-332\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, you can give a name to the new email notification. The idea behind the input mask is to keep the data entry&nbsp;process somewhat uniform and minimize the chances for incorrect data to be entered into the field. You use an input mask when it\u2019s important that the format of the input values is consistent.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">&nbsp;For example, you might use an input mask with a field that stores phone numbers so that Access requires ten digits of input. If someone enters digit more or less than ten digits, Access won\u2019t write the data until ten digits is added.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Custom Input Mask Rules<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>9:<\/strong>Numeric (0-9)<\/li><li><strong>a:<\/strong>Alphabetical (a-z or A-Z)<\/li><li><strong>A:<\/strong>Uppercase alphabetical (A-Z)<\/li><li><strong>*:<\/strong>Alphanumeric (0-9, a-z, or A-Z)<\/li><li><strong>&amp;:<\/strong>Uppercase alphanumeric (0-9 or A-Z)<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>How to Add Custom Input Mask<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>You must create new form or edit an existing form to add custom input mask.<\/li><li>Input masks can only be added to Single Line Text field.<\/li><li>Drag and Drop Single Line Text.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2018\/07\/single_line_text.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-741\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>After dragging Single Line Text click on click on the form field on the right side to open up the&nbsp;<strong>Field Options.<\/strong><\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2018\/07\/click.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-738\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>In Field option go to Advance Option. In advance field option you can see Input Mask option.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2018\/07\/input_option.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-739\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can now mask input from here.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For example, if you\u2019d like to require a 10-digit number you could enter ten&nbsp;9\u2018s into the Input Mask field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2018\/07\/output.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-740\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When the form is displayed on a page and a user clicks in the field, an underscore will be shown for each required digit to help guide the user. Additionally, since we used the&nbsp;9&nbsp;symbol, only numeric values will be allowed (no letters or other symbols). You can add alpha numeric value to mask input as mention above.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Additionally you can include symbols like hyphens (-) or parenthesis in your input mask.<\/strong><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":57,"slug":"how-to-show-or-hide-the-submit-button","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-show-or-hide-the-submit-button\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Show or Hide the Submit Button?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Show or Hide Submit Button in Everest Forms\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/zu6uLQkX3hI?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">There are many ways to make your forms more <strong>interactive<\/strong> to the user inputs. The AND\/OR conditional logic allows you to implement more complex conditions to display or hide your form fields.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> In this tutorial, you&#8217;ll learn how to apply AND\/OR conditional logic on your forms with no trouble.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">AND Conditional Logic<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you use <strong>AND<\/strong> conditional logic then, you can choose to show or hide any field if two or more conditions apply. If the user inputs match with all the conditions connected by the AND logic only then, the field is shown or hidden.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, we are going to take a look at an example for AND conditional logic in forms:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this example, the &#8220;Email&#8221; Field shows <strong>only if both the conditions<\/strong> i.e &#8220;Want us to contact you? = Yes&#8221; <strong>AND<\/strong> &#8220;Communication Medium = Email&#8221; <strong>is selected<\/strong> in the form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-and.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-321\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly,  the &#8220;Phone&#8221; Field shows <strong>only if both the conditions<\/strong> i.e &#8220;Want us to contact you? = Yes&#8221; <strong>AND<\/strong> &#8220;Communication Medium = Phone&#8221; <strong>is selected<\/strong> in the form.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-or.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-322\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Conditional-logic-AND.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-323\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"mce_5\">OR Conditional Logic<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In <strong>OR<\/strong> conditional logic, you can show or hide any field if one of the two or more conditions is correct. If the user input matches with one of the conditions connected by the OR logic then, the field is shown or hidden.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, we are going to take a look at an example for OR conditional logic in forms: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this example, the &#8220;Email&#8221; Field shows <strong>if one of the conditions<\/strong> i.e &#8220;Want us to contact you? = Yes&#8221; <strong>OR<\/strong> &#8220;Communication Medium = Email&#8221; <strong>is selected<\/strong> in the form.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/Conditional-Logic-OR-1-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-324\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> Similarly,  the &#8220;Phone&#8221; Field shows<strong> if one of the conditions<\/strong> i.e &#8220;Want us to contact you? = Yes&#8221; <strong>OR<\/strong> &#8220;Communication Medium = Phone&#8221; <strong>is selected<\/strong> in the form.   <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-325\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-docs-or.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-326\"\/><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":51,"slug":"how-to-implement-conditional-logic-in-form-fields","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-implement-conditional-logic-in-form-fields\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Implement Conditional Logic in Form Fields?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> allows you to enable and implement <strong>Conditional Logic<\/strong> on each form field. Implementing conditional logic allows you to create intuitive forms for your site. But, don&#8217;t you think it is a complex process. It can be easily integrated with the help of this plugin. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to Everest Forms and open the form you want to use conditional logic. Then, click on a Form Field you want to edit. In the Field Options tab below the Advanced Options, you can see the Conditional logic Settings. Here, you need to set the condition rules for form field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/condtional-logic-everest-forms.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-310\" width=\"1158\" height=\"812\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To understand how conditional logic works in Everest Forms, you need to know the settings in the conditional logic section in the forms. These are the options that allow you to create conditional rules that you require.<\/p>\n\n\n<ul id=\"mce_11\" class=\"editor-rich-text__tinymce mce-content-body\" role=\"textbox\" contenteditable=\"true\" aria-label=\"Write list\u2026\" aria-autocomplete=\"list\" aria-multiline=\"true\" data-is-placeholder-visible=\"false\">\n<li><strong>Show\/Hide:<\/strong> This option allows you to Show or Hide the form fields according to the rule you set.<\/li>\n<li><strong>Select Field:<\/strong> In this option, you need to select a field in your form which determines the logic.<\/li>\n<li><strong>Comparison:<\/strong> You need to choose the comparison to compare the user input. There are Six comparisons that you can choose from and they are: is, is not, empty, not empty, greater than, and less than.<img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" class=\"alignnone size-medium wp-image-312\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/conditional-logic-comparisions-1-300x229.png\" alt=\"\" width=\"300\" height=\"229\" \/><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<p><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" class=\"aligncenter wp-image-3423 size-full\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/wp-content\/uploads\/2019\/06\/conditional-logic-comparisions.png\" alt=\"\" width=\"388\" height=\"296\" \/><\/p>\n<ul id=\"mce_11\" class=\"editor-rich-text__tinymce mce-content-body\" role=\"textbox\" contenteditable=\"true\" aria-label=\"Write list\u2026\" aria-autocomplete=\"list\" aria-multiline=\"true\" data-is-placeholder-visible=\"false\">\n<li><strong>Select Choice:<\/strong> Here, you need to enter the value that you want the user to fill in and apply the condition. <em>Note: This field will not be used when you are using the empty or not empty comparison<\/em>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, we&#8217;ll show you how to apply Conditional logic for form field with examples.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Case 1: Show\/hide field if the user enters a specific input<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this example, we have a form field called <strong>Subject<\/strong>.&nbsp; If the user enters <strong>Purchase<\/strong> as the field input then, we have to show the <strong>Purchase Items Field<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to the <strong>Field Options<\/strong> of the form field(<strong>Purchase Items<\/strong>) that you want to show and <strong>Enable Conditional Logic<\/strong> for that field. Then, apply the conditional logic rule here.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on <strong>Show<\/strong> and select the form field as <strong>Subject<\/strong>. Let the comparison option be <strong>Is<\/strong> and then, enter the specific input.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-docs-one.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-314\" width=\"1161\" height=\"553\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, save your form and add it to your website and, you can see the condition work.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-form-docs-1-1.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-315\" width=\"1160\" height=\"758\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Case 2: Show different fields for different user inputs on the Multiple Choice Field<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this example, we have a form field called <strong>Communication Medium<\/strong>. And, the options are <strong>Phone<\/strong> or <strong>Email<\/strong>. The user can only choose one.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> If the user selects Phone, we need to show <strong>Phone Field<\/strong>. And if the user selects Email, we need to show <strong>Email Field<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, we go to the <strong>Field Options<\/strong> of <strong>Phone<\/strong> Field and <strong>Enable Conditional Logic<\/strong>. Then, click on <strong>Show <\/strong>and select the field as <strong>Communication Medium<\/strong>. Let the comparison option be <strong>Is<\/strong> and then, select the option as <strong>Phone<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-docs-two.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-316\" width=\"1156\" height=\"702\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Do the same for <strong>Email<\/strong> Field but, don&#8217;t forget to choose the option as <strong>Email.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">\n\nThen, save your form and add it to your website and, you can see the condition work.\n\n<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-form-docs-one-.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-317\" width=\"1161\" height=\"532\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Case 3: Show hidden fields on a single click of the users<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> In this example, we have a form field called <strong>Other Information<\/strong> and it consists of a checkbox field called <strong>Work Experience<\/strong>. We have to show Field like Company name, Position, Date\/Time<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, we go to the <strong>Field Options<\/strong> of Field and <strong>Enable Conditional Logic<\/strong>. Then, click on <strong>Show <\/strong>and select the field as <strong>Other Information<\/strong>. Let the comparison option be <strong>Is<\/strong> and then, select the option as <strong>Work Experience<\/strong>.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-docs-three-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-318\" width=\"1160\" height=\"681\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"> Then, save your form and add it to your website and, you can see the condition work.  <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/conditional-logic-form-docs-two.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-319\" width=\"1159\" height=\"744\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":49,"slug":"how-to-create-gdpr-compliant-form","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-create-gdpr-compliant-form\/","title":{"rendered":"How to create GDPR compliant form?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms is GDPR compliant. You can create a GDPR compliant form. Please follow this blog tutorial on how to easily create a GDPR compliant form<br><a rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/blog\/how-to-create-gdpr-compliant-forms\/#everest-forms\" target=\"_blank\">GDPR compliant Everest Forms<\/a><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":47,"slug":"how-to-integrate-google-recaptcha","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-google-recaptcha\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Integrate Google reCAPTCHA in WordPress?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Protect your WordPress forms from spam and bot submissions by integrating Google reCAPTCHA with Everest Forms. This comprehensive guide walks you through the entire setup process, from obtaining API keys to testing your implementation.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Prerequisites:\n- <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/installing-a-plugin\/\">Everest Forms Free or Pro installed and activated<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Understanding reCAPTCHA Versions<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms supports both major reCAPTCHA versions, each offering different user experiences and security levels:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">reCAPTCHA v2 Options<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Checkbox Version (&#8220;I&#8217;m not a robot&#8221;)<\/strong>:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Requires users to click a checkbox actively.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Provides clear visual confirmation of human interaction.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Ideal for sites where explicit user verification is desired.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Invisible Version<\/strong>:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Operates silently in the background.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Only presents challenges when suspicious activity is detected.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Perfect balance between protection and usability.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">reCAPTCHA v3 (Score-Based)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Analyzes user behavior patterns continuously.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Assigns risk scores from 0.0 (bot) to 1.0 (human).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Recommended for high-traffic sites prioritizing seamless user experience.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Obtain Google reCAPTCHA API Keys<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Access the reCAPTCHA Console<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Navigate to the <a href=\"https:\/\/cloud.google.com\/security\/products\/recaptcha\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Google reCAPTCHA <\/a>and sign in with your Google account.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the<strong> Get Started<\/strong> button to register a new site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/get-started-with-recaptcha.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"553\" class=\"wp-block-heading\">Register Your WordPress Site<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Complete the registration form with the following details:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Label<\/strong>: Enter a recognizable name for your project (e.g., &#8220;EVF&#8221;)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>reCAPTCHA Type<\/strong>: Choose between:\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>reCAPTCHA v3<\/strong> (Score-based analysis)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>reCAPTCHA v2<\/strong> (Challenge-based verification)<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Domains<\/strong>: Add your website domain(s) without protocol.\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Example: <code>yoursite.com<\/code> and <code>www.yoursite.com<\/code><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Project Settings<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Google Cloud Platform Project<\/strong>: Create or select an existing project.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After successful registration, you&#8217;ll receive two essential keys:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Site Key (Public)<\/strong>: Used in your website&#8217;s frontend code.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Secret Key (Private)<\/strong>: Used for server-side verification.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/copy-and-paste-site-key-and-secret-key.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"871\" class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Important Security Note<\/strong>: Never expose your Secret Key in frontend code or public repositories. Copy and keep these keys safe to use later. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Configure reCAPTCHA in Everest Forms<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Log in to your WordPress admin dashboard and navigate to <strong>Everest Forms > Settings > Security > Integration.<\/strong> Click on the CAPTCHA tab and configure reCAPTCHA:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>CAPTCHA Type<\/strong>: Select &#8220;reCAPTCHA&#8221; from the dropdown menu.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>reCAPTCHA Version<\/strong>: Choose the version matching your Google setup.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Site Key<\/strong>: Paste your public Site Key.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Secret Key<\/strong>: Paste your private Secret Key.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">For reCAPTCHA v2<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"450\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1-1024x450.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5425\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1-1024x450.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1-768x338.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1-1536x676.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v2-1.jpg 1889w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Invisible reCAPTCHA<\/strong>: Enable this option to use the invisible version of v2.\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>When enabled, the reCAPTCHA runs in the background.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Users only see challenges when the system detects suspicious behavior.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Provides a better user experience compared to the checkbox version.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With this invisible reCAPTCHA, no user interaction is required at all. Like the \u201cI\u2019m not a robot\u201d reCAPTCHA, Google also analyzes the user\u2019s activity, like typing patterns, mouse movements, and browsing history. The reCAPTCHA can be invoked directly when the user clicks on a native button on the page or via a JavaScript API call.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">For reCAPTCHA v3<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"448\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3-1024x448.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5426\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3-1024x448.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3-300x131.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3-768x336.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3-1536x672.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3-360x157.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-recaptcha-v3.jpg 1903w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Threshold Score<\/strong>: Set the minimum score required (0.1 to 1.0):\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>0.1-0.4<\/strong>: Strict filtering (may block legitimate users).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>0.5<\/strong>: Balanced approach (recommended for most sites).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>0.6-1.0<\/strong>: Lenient filtering (may allow some spam).<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If the score is low, indicating potential bot activity, reCAPTCHA may prompt the user to complete additional challenges, like selecting specific pictures. Conversely, if the score is high, close to 1, it&#8217;s likely a genuine human interaction, and reCAPTCHA allows the user to continue without further interruption.Therefore, the lower the score, the more likely the user will need to prove they&#8217;re human.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Additional Settings:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>CAPTCHA Language<\/strong>: Select from the dropdown for a consistent experience.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Review all settings for accuracy and click <strong>Save Settings<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3: Add reCAPTCHA to Your Forms<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; All Forms<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Select the form you want to protect.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the form builder, locate the <strong>Advanced Fields<\/strong> For this, you can use any multilingual plugins such as WPML or Polylang. Here, we are using the WPML plugin which is the most popular Multilingual plugin for WordPress. (Pro Feature)<\/a><\/li>\n<li><a style=\"font-size: inherit;\" href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/guide-to-form-entries\/#13-toc-title\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">How to Edit Form Entries? (Pro Feature)<\/a><\/li>\n<li>How to Sort the column items on the Entry Page?<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"one\"><strong>How to View the Form Entries?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">One of the best features that Everest Forms offers is the dedicated tab for form entries. To access it, go to <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> and click on the <strong>Entries<\/strong> tab.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, you can see all the form entries to your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/View-Entries.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"710\" height=\"245\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/View-Entries.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5564\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/View-Entries.png 710w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/View-Entries-300x104.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/View-Entries-360x124.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 710px) 100vw, 710px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Heartbeat notifications<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>Entries<\/strong> page, you can see if there are new form entries. The form submissions that are submitted after the page is loaded are notified here.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Because of this useful feature, you won&#8217;t miss any new form entries.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1878\" height=\"841\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5461\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1.jpg 1878w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1-300x134.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1-1024x459.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1-768x344.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1-1536x688.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-heart-beat-api-1-360x161.jpg 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1878px) 100vw, 1878px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Another way of accessing the form entries is from individual forms.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and you\u2019ll see all the forms that you created. Then, hover over the form you want. You\u2019ll see many options for the individual form there.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-all-forms-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1885\" height=\"806\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-all-forms-entries.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5462\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-all-forms-entries-1024x438.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-all-forms-entries-768x328.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-all-forms-entries-360x154.jpg 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1885px) 100vw, 1885px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, click on the <strong>Entries<\/strong> option and you\u2019ll see all the form entries for the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To view all the information of a single entry, Click on the <strong>View<\/strong> option from <strong>Actions<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-view-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"455\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-view-entries-768x341.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-view-entries-1536x682.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-view-entries-360x160.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-view-entries.jpg 1897w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"two\"><strong>How to Filter Form Entries by Form?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you create few forms for your site, you can filter your form entries by respective forms. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">First, go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Entries<\/strong> and on the top of the form entries, there is the <strong>Filter <\/strong>button. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">There is a <strong>drop-down<\/strong> option right beside the <strong>Filter<\/strong> button where you can choose any form. So, choose the form that you want to see the form entries of. Then, click on <strong>Filter<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"450\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms-1024x450.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5465\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms-1024x450.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms-768x337.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms-1536x674.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms-360x158.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-entries-filter-by-forms.jpg 1895w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"three\"><strong>How to Search for Form Entries?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can even search for specific entries if you need. There is a <strong>Search<\/strong> bar at the <strong>top-right<\/strong> of the form entries. You can search for any entry by name or subject.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"450\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries-1024x450.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5466\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries-1024x450.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries-768x338.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries-1536x675.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries-360x158.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-search-entries.jpg 1892w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"four\"><strong>How to Export Form Entries?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, there is the <strong>Export CSV<\/strong> option. You can export all the form entries to your computer. Just click on the button to export entries.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"453\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv-1024x453.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5467\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv-1024x453.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv-300x133.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv-768x340.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv-1536x679.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-export-entries-into-csv.jpg 1891w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want a more detailed tutorial on this, check out our tutorial on <a href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/blog\/export-wordpress-form-entries\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" aria-label=\" (opens in a new tab)\">How to Export Form Entries to CSV file in WordPress?<\/a><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"five\"><strong>How to Delete Form Entries?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, you can delete the entries from your site. To delete a single entry, you can simply click on the <strong>Trash<\/strong> option in the <strong>Actions<\/strong> section.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"451\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries-1024x451.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5470\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries-1024x451.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries-768x338.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries-1536x676.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries-360x158.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-trash-entries.jpg 1890w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To delete selective entries, select the entries and select <strong>Trash<\/strong> in the Bulk action option and click on <strong>Apply<\/strong>. Then, your selected entries will be deleted.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"453\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1-1024x453.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5469\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1-1024x453.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1-300x133.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1-768x339.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1-1536x679.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-bulk-action-trash-1.jpg 1889w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you can also delete all the form entries in one go. For this, select the Checkbox next to Name. This automatically selects all the form entries. After that, select <strong>Trash<\/strong> from the bulk action option and click on <strong>Apply<\/strong>. <br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"six\"><strong>How to Hide Empty Fields?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, there is the option to hide empty fields in a form entry. If you click on any entry, you can see the Hide Empty Field option in the top right corner of the entry. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"448\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field-1024x448.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5472\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field-1024x448.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field-300x131.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field-768x336.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field-1536x673.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field-360x158.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-hide-empty-entries-field.jpg 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can use this feature to hide the fields that are left empty. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"seven\"><strong>How to Disable Form Entries?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Furthermore, you can disable form entries from individual forms whenever you like. Go to the form builder and click on <strong>Settings.<\/strong> <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the General settings, if you scroll down a bit, you\u2019ll see the checkbox option to disable storing entry information. Click on that to <strong>disable form entries to your form<\/strong>.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"450\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries-1024x450.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5474\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries-1024x450.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries-768x338.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries-1536x676.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries-360x158.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-disable-storing-entries.jpg 1885w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"eight\"><strong>How to Restore Trashed Form Entries?<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you accidentally delete entries then, don\u2019t panic. You can retrieve these entries from the trash. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to Entries and you\u2019ll find a <strong>Trash<\/strong> section on the top of the entries page. After you click on it, you\u2019ll see the deleted entries there. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">So, you can either <strong>Restore<\/strong> it or <strong>Delete Permanently<\/strong>.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"452\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries-1024x452.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5475\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries-1024x452.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries-768x339.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries-1536x677.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-restore-entries.jpg 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"nine\"><strong>How to Star Form Entries? (Pro Feature)<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you can add the form entries to starred so, you can keep track of the important form entries. Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Entries<\/strong>. Then, select the Form you want to view the entries.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, you can see the star option for each form entry.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"452\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries-1024x452.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5476\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries-1024x452.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries-768x339.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries-1536x678.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-star-entries.jpg 1892w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you add the form entries in Starred, you can easily view only the Starred form entries. For this, click on the <strong>Starred<\/strong> tab on the top left of the Entries page.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-starred-entry-list.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"448\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-starred-entry-list-1024x448.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5477\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-starred-entry-list-360x157.jpg 360w,
