class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, when you view the individual entries, you have the option to Unstar the form entry whenever you like.&nbsp;<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"ten\"><strong>How to Make the Form Entries Unread? (Pro Feature)<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Pro version of the plugin will allow you to make the form entries Unread. For this simply click on the small Green circle on each form entry. And just like the Starred option, you can view the Unread form entries in one place through the <strong>Unread<\/strong> tab.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"453\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry-1024x453.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5479\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry-1024x453.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry-300x133.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry-768x340.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry-1536x680.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-unread-entry.jpg 1895w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"eleven\"><strong>How to Resend Notification for Individual Form Entries? (Pro Feature)<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the Everest Forms (Pro), you can resend notifications for each individual form. For this, go to Everest Forms \u2192 Entries. Then, you can view any form entry and click <strong>Resend Notifications<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"451\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification-1024x451.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5480\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification-1024x451.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification-768x338.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification-1536x677.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-resend-entry-notification.jpg 1891w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to Print Individual Form Entry? (Pro Feature) <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the Everest Forms (Pro), you can print each individual form. For this, go to Everest Forms \u2192 Entries. Then, you can view any form entry and click <strong>Print<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"452\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries-1024x452.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5481\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries-1024x452.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries-768x339.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries-1536x678.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries-360x159.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-print-entries.jpg 1894w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How To Edit Form Entries? (Pro Feature)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now you can also <strong>Edit the Form Entries<\/strong> that you received from your users. To do so, <strong>Click <\/strong>on the <strong>View link<\/strong> on the entry that you want to edit. After the <strong>Entry Details<\/strong> are shown, you will see the section highlighted in the screenshot.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"449\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2-1024x449.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5485\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2-1024x449.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2-300x132.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2-768x337.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2-1536x674.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2-360x158.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entries-2.jpg 1887w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on the <strong>Edit<\/strong> <strong>button <\/strong>and the <strong>field values<\/strong> will show up in the <strong>input boxes<\/strong>. After that, <strong>change the data<\/strong> that you want to change and then <strong>click on Save<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view.jpg\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"454\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view-1024x454.jpg\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5486\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view-1024x454.jpg 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view-300x133.jpg 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view-768x340.jpg 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view-1536x681.jpg 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view-360x160.jpg 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/everest-forms-edit-entry-view.jpg 1897w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">All Changes to the Entry Will be saved. Now, you can successfully edit the form entries this way. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">You can use the Next\/Previous button to go to another entry from the single entry page.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How right-most section of the column title. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"451\" style=\"width:790px;height:400px\"\/><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[6]},{"id":41,"slug":"how-to-use-smart-tags","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-use-smart-tags\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Use Smart Tags in Everest Forms?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Smart Tags are dynamic placeholders that automatically insert real-time information into your forms and emails. They&#8217;re special codes enclosed in curly braces <code>{}<\/code> that replace themselves with actual data, like <code>{user_name}<\/code> showing the current user&#8217;s name or <code>{current_date}<\/code> displaying today&#8217;s date.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Simple Example<\/strong>: If you have a contact form with Name and Message fields, instead of receiving emails with generic subjects like &#8220;New Form Entry&#8221;, Smart Tags let you create subjects like &#8220;Message from {field_id=&#8221;name&#8221;}: {field_id=&#8221;subject&#8221;}&#8221; which automatically becomes &#8220;Message from John Smith: Website Bug Report&#8221; &#8211; telling you exactly who contacted you and why.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Use Smart Tags?\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/TR98Lmxpg50?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Reusing Field Values with Smart Tags <\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can now use Smart Tags to copy values from one field to another automatically. This is perfect when you need the same information in multiple places but only want users to enter it once.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How It Works<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you have two fields like &#8220;Official Name&#8221; and &#8220;Name&#8221;, you can:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Let users fill in the &#8220;Name&#8221; field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Automatically copy that value to a hidden &#8220;Official Name&#8221; field using Smart Tags.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Users only see and fill one field, but both get the same data.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup Steps<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Create your fields<\/strong>: Add both the main field (visible) and the secondary field (will be hidden).<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"535\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field-1024x535.png\" alt=\"Add Primary and Secondary Field\" class=\"wp-image-5227\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field-1024x535.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field-300x157.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field-768x401.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field-360x188.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/add-primary-and-secondary-field.png 1325w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">2. <strong>Configure the secondary field<\/strong>:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on the field and navigate to Field Option on the left<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Advanced Options<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click<strong> the smart tag button &#8220;&lt;>&#8221;<\/strong> under the <strong>Default Value<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Select the main field from where you want to auto-populate the value (e.g., <code>{field_id=\"name_ys0GeZISRs-1\"}<\/code>).<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"613\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate-1024x613.png\" alt=\"Click Smart Tag Button to Auto Populate\" class=\"wp-image-5228\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate-1024x613.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate-300x180.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate-768x460.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate-360x215.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/click-smart-tag-button-to-auto-populate.png 1315w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Enable the <strong>Hidden<\/strong> option.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"478\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field-1024x478.png\" alt=\"Hide Field\" class=\"wp-image-5229\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field-1024x478.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field-300x140.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field-768x359.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field-360x168.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/hide-field.png 1300w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Save and test<\/strong>: The hidden field will automatically populate with the main field&#8217;s value.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code><strong>Important<\/strong>: The field that reuses values must be hidden from users.<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Testing Your Setup<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Submit a test form to check that hidden fields don&#8217;t show up at the frontend. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image Notifications<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Let&#8217;s say you have a form with the following fields for your site.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Name<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Email<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Product Name<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Subject<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Message<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"804\" height=\"515\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/everest-subject.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-267\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-subject.png 804w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-subject-300x192.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-subject-768x492.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-subject-360x231.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 804px) 100vw, 804px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Every time a user comes to your site, fills out the form, and submits it, the admin receives an email with all these details. The subject of the email by default would be <strong>New Form Entry<\/strong>. This is not very helpful, and the admin has to open up the email to know the details of the entries.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"534\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/everest-smart-tags-1024x534.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-268\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-smart-tags-1024x534.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-smart-tags-300x157.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-smart-tags-768x401.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-smart-tags-360x188.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/everest-smart-tags.png 1198w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now with the help of a smart tag. You can make your email subject dynamic by utilizing the Product Name field and the Subject Field. To\u00a0use the smart tag.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on the<strong> &lt; &gt; icon<\/strong> on the side of the Email Subject field. It will open a small scrollable box with all the appropriate fields in your form that can be used as a smart tag.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, click on the field in the box that you would like to use as a smart tag, and the corresponding smart tag code will be automatically copied to the Email Subject Field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>For this\u00a0article case, I have used the Product Name Field and the Subject field. Your new email subject would be <strong>New Form Entry + Product Name + Subject<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This subject is more elaborate and appropriate.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, if some user fills up the form with the following details as shown in the image below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"631\" height=\"729\" which product the email is related to and what the issue is with a glance at their inbox without the need to open the email.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img Tags<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>You can use the following smart tags while modifying the email subject and contents:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p to Import\/Export Forms?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">There are many times you may need to export your forms from one site to another. Now, <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> allows you to easily export and import your forms with the help of the <strong>Import\/Export<\/strong> tool. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">So, we are going to show you how to import and export your forms with easy instructions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<hr class=\"wp-block-separator has-css-opacity\"\/>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to Export Forms?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, you can now export any <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> forms to your computer. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to the <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> and click on the <strong>Tools<\/strong> tab. Now, you\u2019ll see there are three tabs: <em>Import, Export, and Logs<\/em>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/image-1.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/image-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2649\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to the <strong>Export<\/strong> tab and choose a form you want to export. Then, click on the <strong>Export<\/strong> button and, the form will be instant to your computer. The downloaded form will be saved in a JSON file.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: You can export multiple forms at a time.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/image-1-2.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/02\/image-1-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2650\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to Import Forms?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this, go to the <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> and click on the <strong>Tools<\/strong> tab. Now, you\u2019ll see there are three tabs: <em>Import, Export, Form Migrator and System Info<\/em>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to the <strong>Import<\/strong> tab and you\u2019ll see the <strong>Choose File<\/strong> option. Click on it and choose the Form file that you want to import to <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong>.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After the file is selected, click on the <strong>Import Form<\/strong> button on the bottom-right corner.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/Import-Form-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1280\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Your form will be successfully imported in no time. To check if the form is imported or not, either click on &#8216;<strong>View Form<\/strong>&#8216; or go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong>. Here, you\u2019ll find the newly imported file.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/03\/View-Form-1-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1277\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh3.googleusercontent.com\/gXH0zKkw2GZ1GB6my7yzeLx7pmEvMEa0FGNkiCFOFac6UL81koQET6q6I6ynIXaPMKaW1fVzHJCl6gy9dbdxR0jTC8r7xNxK_1smkjnqRX3fM9nGgA_PxMLGeKtp6_Zb0owUUUmZ\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: You can import multiple forms at a time. Now, you\u2019ll see there are three tabs:&nbsp;<em>Import, Export, Form Migrator and System Info<\/em>. You will see the option to map CSV Fields with Form Fields, as shown below. You can configure unlimited email notifications. For <g class=\"gr_ gr_15 gr-alert gr_gramm gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim Punctuation only-del replaceWithoutSep\" id=\"15\" data-gr-id=\"15\">example<\/g> Admin Email Notification that is triggered to admin, User Notification email that is triggered to the user who fills up the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">First, you need to <strong>turn on<\/strong> the Email settings to start sending Email notifications with Everest Forms.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"365\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings-1024x365.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3469\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings-1024x365.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings-300x107.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings-768x274.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings-1536x547.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings-360x128.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Turn-On-Email-Settings.png 1684w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Emails Settings allows you to configure &#8220;To&#8221; and &#8220;From&#8221; email addresses for the emails. You can also customize the subject and add additional messages attached to the forms if you wish. You can access the Email Settings Page from your individual Form settings tab.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><a href=\"#admin-email-notification\">Setting Up Admin Email Notification<\/a><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><a href=\"#user-email-notification\">Setting Up User Email Notification<\/a><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"487\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main-1024x487.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3470\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main-1024x487.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main-300x143.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main-768x365.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main-1536x730.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main-360x171.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Email-Main.png 1683w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"admin-email-notification\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#admin-email-notification\">\n<h3 class=\"inline\">Setting up Admin Email Notification<\/h3>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">By default, an Admin  Notification email setup is already created on any form you create. However, you need to check the email settings to be sure that emails are delivered correctly. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this go to your individual <strong>Form \u2014&gt;Settings\u2014&gt;Email\u2014&gt;Admin Notification.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"588\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification-1024x588.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3471\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification-1024x588.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification-300x172.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification-768x441.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification-360x207.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Admin-Notification.png 1119w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"to-address\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#to-address\">\n<h4 class=\"inline\">To Address:<\/h4>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To Address refers to the email address where you would like this email notification to be delivered once a user submits the form. Since we are setting up an admin notification email we enter the admin email here. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p><\/p><blockquote class=\"wp-block-quote\"><p>You can add more than one email address by separating each email with comma<\/p><\/blockquote><p><\/p>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"from-name\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#from-name\">\n<h4 class=\"inline\">From Name:<\/h4>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From Name enables you to add the desired Name to be displayed when sending the form. Basically, if the email is being triggered to Admin then you may want to add the user&#8217;s name who filled the form. It is possible to dynamically grab the Name by using Smart Tags. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"931\" height=\"209\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/smart-tags-from-name.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-263\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you are using smart tag make sure you have the Name field required in your form else this might be empty if the user doesn&#8217;t fill up their name in the form. Empty From N<g class=\"gr_ gr_37 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"37\" data-gr-id=\"37\">ame<\/g> might create an issue in email delivery. If the email is being delivered to the user for e.g. User Notification email, you may want to add your company name or a site name in the From Name.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"from-address\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#from-address\">\n<h4 class=\"inline\">From Address:<\/h4>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From address refers to the email address from where the forms are sent. For this, you can use your admin email<\/p>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"reply-to\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#reply-to\">\n<h4 class=\"inline\">Reply To:<\/h4>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><g class=\"gr_ gr_4 gr-alert gr_spell gr_inline_cards gr_run_anim ContextualSpelling ins-del multiReplace\" id=\"4\" data-gr-id=\"4\">Reply To<\/g> address refers to the email address where you would like the email to be replied to when the email receiver clicks on the reply in their email service. Since we are setting up admin email notification then you may want to set the reply address to the email address of the user who filled up the form. You can do this by using the smart tags as instructed in the  From Name above. <br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If this is user notification email then you may want to set reply-to address to admin email. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"email-subject\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#email-subject\">\n<h4 class=\"inline\">Email Subject:<\/h4>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can add the subject of the emails sent along with the form if you want right here.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"email-message\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#email-message\">\n<h4 class=\"inline\">Email Message:<\/h4>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Any specific message that you would like to send each time on the form can be written here. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>{all_fields}<\/strong> smart tag can be used to display all the form fields information filled by the user. This is very handy if you as an admin would like to receive all the field information to be delivered to your email address.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Email Preview<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can preview the email you have added with the help of the preview button on the side of the email notification name.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/EmailPreview.gif\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"600\" height=\"284\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/EmailPreview.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3474\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Email Duplicate<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can duplicate the email notification if you just have to make minor changes to the new notification. Just click on the duplicate icon and give the desired name to the duplicated notification.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"377\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email-1024x377.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3475\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email-1024x377.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email-300x110.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email-768x283.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email-1536x566.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email-360x133.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Duplicate-Email.png 1667w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<div id=\"user-email-notification\" class=\"clipboard\" data-copytarget=\"#user-email-notification\">\n<h3 class=\"inline\">Setting up User Email Notification:<\/h3>\n<\/div>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Sending a confirmation email to your user is a great way to connect with your visitors who filled out the form. You can do this by setting up a user notification email.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For this simply click on the <strong>Add New Email<\/strong> button. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"406\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-1024x406.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3472\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-1024x406.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-300x119.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-768x304.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-1536x609.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1-360x143.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/02\/Add-New-Email-1.png 1670w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A popup will appear, give it a name. For e.g let&#8217;s name it User Email Notification. Then click OK<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"428\" height=\"326\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/email-notification-popup.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-265\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/email-notification-popup.png 428w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/email-notification-popup-300x229.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/email-notification-popup-360x274.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 428px) 100vw, 428px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now simply start filling in the To Address, From Address, Reply To Address, and other details.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>To, Address<\/strong> use smart tag to grab the email address of the person who filled the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>From Name,<\/strong> you can enter your site name or your business name<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For the <strong>From Address,<\/strong> you can use your admin email<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the <strong>Reply To<\/strong> enter the admin email. This is the email address where you would like your users to reply to.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, fill in the Email Subject and Email Message. If you want to send a copy of all the form field details to the user who filled the form, then you can use the {all_fields} smart tags.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send File with Email: [Pro]<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can send the files uploaded by the users from the fields like file upload, image upload, and signature as an attachment with the email. For this, please select the option &#8216;Send File as Attachment&#8217; located at the bottom of the Email settings for each form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/05\/Send-Files-in-Email-EVF-Pro-1-1024x471.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2719\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Send CSV File as Attachment: <\/strong>Enable this option to send the form entries as a CSV file to the email recipient. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Smart Tags<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>You can use the following smart tags while modifying the email subject and contents:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p Settings"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The General Settings in the <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> consist of the basic form settings such as Form Name, Submission Message, Layout Design, Submit Button settings, and so on.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">We have explained each setting so you can understand its functionalities.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">General<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"539\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings-1024x539.png\" alt=\"Everest Forms General Settings\" class=\"wp-image-5060\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings-1024x539.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings-300x158.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings-768x404.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings-1536x808.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/everest-forms-general-settings.png 1686w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><strong>Form Name:<\/strong> From here, you can give your Form a name, such as Contact Form<\/span>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Form Description:<\/strong> Here, you can add a description to your form to let users know about the purpose of your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Tags<\/strong> <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>New<\/strong><\/span>: Here, you can add tags to your form to<span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\"> identify the form with the tag when you view the forms through\u00a0<strong>Everest Forms > All Forms<\/strong><\/span>. Once you add a tag to the form, you can also reuse the tags in other forms.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"471\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form-1024x471.png\" alt=\"Example of Using Tags to Form\" class=\"wp-image-5069\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form-1024x471.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form-300x138.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form-768x353.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form-1536x706.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form-360x166.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-using-tags-to-form.png 1583w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Form disabled message:<\/strong> This text is displayed when you disable the form using the toggle button from <strong>Everest Forms &gt; All Forms<\/strong> tab. You can check out our docs for a detailed guide on how to disable a form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Layout Design:<\/strong> Everest Forms provides two options for choosing the layout of your form: Default and Classic. You can choose the one that you&#8217;re comfortable with. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Form Class: <\/strong>Here,<span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> you can add <\/span>a <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">class to the created form. This class attribute can be used to style your form. <\/span> You can add CSS class names for the form wrapper. To add multiple class names, you can separate them with spaces. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Required Indicators<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Required Indicator allows you to select a type of required indicator in the form. Currently, it supports three types of required indicators.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"357\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms-1024x357.png\" alt=\"Required Indicators Settings for Everest Forms\" class=\"wp-image-5061\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms-1024x357.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms-300x105.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms-768x268.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms-360x125.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/required-indicators-settings-for-everest-forms.png 1412w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Asterisk(*): <\/strong>This is the default required indicator for the form fields. If you have set the field as required, then Asterisk(*) appears to the side of that respective field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Required: <\/strong>If you choose Required Indicator as a &#8220;Required&#8221;, the &#8220;Required&#8221; text comes instead of the Asterisk in the field you have made required.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Custom Text: <\/strong>If you choose the Required Indicator as a &#8220;Custom text,&#8221; then you can add the custom text according to your choice in the Required fields.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Submit Button<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"507\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms-1024x507.png\" alt=\"Submit Button Settings for Everest Forms\" class=\"wp-image-5062\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms-1024x507.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms-300x149.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms-768x380.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms-360x178.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/submit-button-settings-for-everest-forms.png 1423w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Submit button text:<\/strong> Change the text of the submit button. You can enter &#8216;Submit&#8217;, &#8216;Send&#8217;, &#8216;Contact Us&#8217;, or anything appropriate to the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Submit button processing text: <\/strong>Text is displayed on the button when the form is processed.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Submit button class: <\/strong>Enter the CSS class names for the submit button\u2014separate multiple classes with spaces.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Conditional Logic: <\/strong>Toggle to enable conditional logic on the form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can hide, show, or disable the submit button using the conditional logic feature in the pro version. For a detailed guide, check this tutorial on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-show-or-hide-the-submit-button\/\">how to show or hide the submit button<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">External Upload Path<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The External Upload Path settings allow you to configure where files uploaded through your form will be stored.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"428\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms-1024x428.png\" alt=\"External Upload Path Settings for Everest Forms\" class=\"wp-image-5063\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms-1024x428.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms-300x126.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms-768x321.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms-360x151.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/external-upload-path-settings-for-everest-forms.png 1410w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Dropbox File path:<\/strong> Toggle this option to enable file uploads directly to your Dropbox account. All files submitted through this form will be stored in your connected Dropbox account when enabled.<br><strong>Note:<\/strong> Files will be uploaded to the custom directory <code>\/Apps\/Everest Forms\/<\/code> in your Dropbox by default. This helps keep your form uploads organized in a dedicated folder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Google Drive File path:<\/strong> Toggle this option to enable file uploads directly to your Google Drive account. When enabled, all files submitted through this form will be stored in your connected Google Drive account.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Ajax Form Submission: <\/strong>Toggle this option to enable asynchronous file uploads. This improves the user experience by allowing files to be uploaded in the background without requiring a full page refresh during form submission.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"419\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms-1024x419.png\" alt=\"Form Settings for Everest Forms\" class=\"wp-image-5064\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms-1024x419.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms-300x123.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms-768x314.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms-360x147.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/form-setttings-for-everest-forms.png 1409w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Disable storing entry information:<\/strong> Check this option to disable saving the form entries in the database. If disabled, the entries will not be saved to <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> <strong>&gt;<\/strong> <strong>Entries<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable entries dashboard analytics:<\/strong> Toggle this option to activate analytics tracking for form submissions. When enabled, this feature provides visual reports and statistics about your form&#8217;s performance, allowing you to monitor submission patterns, completion rates, and other metrics directly in your dashboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable Form Field Icon: <\/strong>Toggle this option to display visual icons within your form fields. When enabled, each field type will show a relevant icon (e.g., envelope for email, calendar for date) to provide visual cues that help users understand the expected input type.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Form-Field-Icons-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2196\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Enable keyboard Friendly Form: <\/strong>Toggle this option to optimize your form for keyboard navigation. When enabled, users can easily tab through fields and submit forms without requiring a mouse, improving accessibility and user experience.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Save these changes once you have completed setting up the general settings for the form. <\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[10]},{"id":25,"slug":"how-to-disable-a-form","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-disable-a-form\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Disable a Form?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Disable a Form in Everest Forms? class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Gutenberg editor, there are two ways to display the forms on your posts and pages.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 1: Using the Everest Forms Block<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">First, click on <strong>Pages &gt; Add Page<\/strong>. Then, click on the <strong>Add block<\/strong> icon and search for Everest Forms in the search bar. Click on the <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> block that appears. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"847\" height=\"523\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/display-form-in-wordpress.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-226\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Display Form Title and Description<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the Everest Forms block editor, you can now choose to display the <strong>Title<\/strong> and a <strong>Description<\/strong> for your forms<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"630\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form-1024x630.png\" alt=\"Title and Description for Form\" class=\"wp-image-5082\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form-1024x630.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form-300x185.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form-768x473.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form-360x222.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/title-and-description-for-form.png 1355w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">But first, you need to add a description to your form. To do this, open your form in Editor, go to&nbsp;<strong>Settings &gt; General<\/strong>,&nbsp;and&nbsp;see the Form description option. You can type in the text you want to describe your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"490\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description-1024x490.png\" alt=\"Navigate to Settings and Add Form Description\" class=\"wp-image-5199\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description-1024x490.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description-300x144.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description-768x368.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description-360x172.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-settings-and-add-form-description.png 1303w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you can enable the title and description on your Everest Forms block editor. The title and description will be shown in your form like this. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"576\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description-1024x576.png\" alt=\"Example of Showing Title and Description\" class=\"wp-image-5087\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description-1024x576.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description-300x169.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description-768x432.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description-1536x865.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description-360x203.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/example-of-showing-title-and-description.png 1558w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code><strong>Note: <\/strong>You can only enable title and description, when you select <strong>Popup<\/strong> <strong>Type <\/strong>as None. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Popup Type <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>New<\/strong><\/span><\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms now offers flexible display options for your forms through the Popup Type feature. This functionality allows you to determine how users will access and interact with your forms, providing three distinct options:<strong> None, Link, Button, or Popup<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"704\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type-1024x704.png\" alt=\"Popup Type\" class=\"wp-image-5086\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type-1024x704.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type-300x206.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type-768x528.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type-360x247.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/popup-type.png 1285w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h5 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Display Options<\/h5>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>None: <\/strong>When you select the None option, the form displays directly on the page without additional triggers or containers. This is the standard, traditional form display where all form fields are immediately visible to users when they load the page.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Link:<\/strong> When you select the Link option, a clickable text link will appear on your page. When users click this link, the form appears in the designated area.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-as-link.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"990\" height=\"601\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-as-link.png\" alt=\"Select Popup Type As Link\" class=\"wp-image-5202\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-as-link.png 990w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-as-link-300x182.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-as-link-768x466.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-as-link-360x219.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 990px) 100vw, 990px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Button: <\/strong>The Button option generates a customizable button on your page. Users can click this button to display the form, providing a more prominent call-to-action than a standard text link.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-for-form-as-button.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"995\" height=\"504\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-for-form-as-button.png\" alt=\"Select Popup type for Form as Button\" class=\"wp-image-5203\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-for-form-as-button.png 995w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-for-form-as-button-300x152.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-for-form-as-button-768x389.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/select-popup-type-for-form-as-button-360x182.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 995px) 100vw, 995px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Popup:<\/strong> Selecting the Popup option creates a modal window over your page content. The form will load directly in a pop-up window without requiring any additional buttons or text interactions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/pop-up-form-example.gif\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1276\" height=\"837\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/pop-up-form-example.gif\" alt=\"Pop up Form Example\" class=\"wp-image-5094\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Popup Type Settings<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the <strong>popup types<\/strong> for your form, you can further customize the appearance and functionality with the following settings:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Popup Button\/Link Text:<\/strong> Customize the default text displayed on the button\/link to reveal the form when clicked.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Popup Size:<\/strong> Set the dimensions of the popup window to ensure it fits your design preferences.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Popup Header Title:<\/strong> Add a title to the header section of the popup window for better context.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Popup<\/strong> <strong>Header Description:<\/strong> To guide users, include a brief description or instructions in the header section.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Popup<\/strong> <strong>Footer Title:<\/strong> Provide a title for the footer section, adding a professional touch to the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong><strong>Popup<\/strong><\/strong> <strong>Footer Description:<\/strong> Add additional information or links in the footer section for a comprehensive user experience.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Additionally, within the <strong>Advanced Settings<\/strong>, you can apply custom CSS classes to further style and enhance the appearance of your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"624\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings-1024x624.png\" alt=\"Popup Type Settings\" class=\"wp-image-5201\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings-1024x624.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings-300x183.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings-768x468.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings-360x219.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/popup-type-settings.png 1198w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 2: Using Shortcode<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to <strong>Everest Forms&gt;&gt;All Forms<\/strong> and Copy the Shortcode of the form you want to display.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Then, in the Gutenberg editor, click on <strong>Add block<\/strong> icon and search for Shortcode on the search bar. Click on the <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> block.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"703\" height=\"481\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/display-wordpress-form.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-232\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/display-wordpress-form.png 703w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/display-wordpress-form-300x205.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/display-wordpress-form-360x246.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 703px) 100vw, 703px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Your form is displayed perfectly on the frontend but it won&#8217;t show in the editor.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"846\" height=\"331\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/wordpress-display-forms.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-233\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/wordpress-display-forms.png 846w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/wordpress-display-forms-300x117.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/wordpress-display-forms-768x300.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/wordpress-display-forms-360x141.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 846px) 100vw, 846px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The form will look something like this on your site. You can customize and rearrange the elements based on your preference.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"739\" height=\"582\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/3\/2020\/06\/display-forms-in-wordpress.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-234\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/display-forms-in-wordpress.png 739w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/display-forms-in-wordpress-300x236.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/06\/display-forms-in-wordpress-360x284.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 739px) 100vw, 739px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">In Elementor<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Display Everest Forms in Elementor\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/oQT9cYYVnVk?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can display the form in the Elementor pages using the available Everest Forms elements.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Search for the &#8220;Everest Forms&#8217; in the Elements section and drag and drop the element to the widget section.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"475\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once dropped on the widget sections, choose the required form from the Content section on the left-hand side.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"391\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Elementor-Final-1024x391.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1380\" 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Elementor-Final-768x294.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Elementor-Final-360x138.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/04\/Elementor-Final.png 1185w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How do you embed the Everest Forms directly from the Form Editor?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"295\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button-1024x295.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button-300x86.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button-768x221.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button-1536x442.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button-360x104.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Button.png 1679w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you create the form and configure its settings, you can directly embed the form to an existing page or to a new page. For this, click on the <strong>Embed <\/strong>button on the top-right of the form builder screen.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you can choose whether to embed the form on an existing page or by creating a new page.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Choose-Page.png\"><img class=\"wp-image-3515\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Choose-Page-1024x375.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Choose-Page-1536x562.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Choose-Page-360x132.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Choose-Page.png 1680w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you select the <strong>Select Existing Page, <\/strong>you can choose one of the pages you have already created in your site and embed the form. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Select-Existing-Page.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"559\" height=\"542\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Select-Existing-Page.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3516\" style=\"width:335px;height:auto\" class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Choose a page and click on <strong>Lets GO!<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">However, if you select <strong>Create New Page<\/strong>, you will need to provide a page&#8217;s name and click on <strong>Lets Go!<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Create-a-New-Page.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"541\" height=\"410\" class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, you will be redirected to the designated page&#8217;s page builder. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2024\/03\/Embed-Add-A-Block.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"252\" to Create a Form with Everest Forms?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With Everest Forms, you can create forms for various purposes for your WordPress site. The user interface is so simple and intuitive so, you\u2019ll be able to create professional and engaging forms in minutes. So let\u2019s get started!<br><br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Create a Form in WordPress With Everest Forms: Easy Tutorial\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/96DEOeDgpwI?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" referrerpolicy=\"strict-origin-when-cross-origin\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you\u2019re done installing and activating Everest Forms on your site, you can see the Everest Forms tab is added to your WordPress admin dashboard menu.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To create a new form, go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Add New<\/strong>. Then, you\u2019ll see the predefined form templates that you can choose form<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"everest-forms-form-templates\">Everest Forms Form Templates<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The plugin offers a set of templates such as Simple and Advanced Contact Form, Application Forms, Appointment Forms, etc.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"1024\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-1024x1024.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4080\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-1024x1024.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-300x300.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-150x150.png 150w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-768x768.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-1536x1536.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-360x360.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms-600x600.png 600w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2020\/05\/Templates-for-New-Forms.png 1604w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Some of the Templates are available in the Free Version of Everest Forms while some are only available for Premium users. And, you can check which template is included for your version by the <strong>Free<\/strong> and <strong>Premium<\/strong> tabs in the top-right of the page.\u00a0\u00a0<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">These templates are created to make your work easier as they contain the form field required for the respective form type. So, if any of the templates look appealing to you, you can go with it.&nbsp;<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">OR, you can <strong>Start From Scratch<\/strong>. This option allows you to create a form from the very beginning so, you can add the form fields that are required.&nbsp;<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh5.googleusercontent.com\/DUGlpvvDweduneQ0E1CHscOjGHB-iH0o7fGBc67XM8jyI6N4mkyv1dDuhcw657dNVlmI20iX5-ymfmj_8GkOZ_D3zGiyOETO4fKRMuDCXPyTb1c0H-BXtEzwz5c2K053uK_y59HP\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you choose a <strong>Template<\/strong> or <strong>Start From Scratch<\/strong>, you need to give the form a Name of your choice. Then, you\u2019ll be taken to the Everest Forms form builder. The drag and drop feature of the form builder allows you to insert any form field and place it wherever you want.&nbsp;&nbsp;<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/lh5.googleusercontent.com\/C3rbhwbSPVQg8NYfAo__wkf5XK0qAbvEUsj8QkDsqBBLbA3IQzrF5k_HA447EWvng48S5MVh8LCYa3l38pAcFhrDLONSQJ4WpGYJjU8Q2iuJhwAe5UUwgen4IqoEmnanYFqSDoTQ\" alt=\"\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the left-hand side of the form builder, you can see the General Field, Advanced Fields, Payment Fields, and Survey Fields. The availability of the form fields is based on your Everest Forms Version. The Free Version offers <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/everest-forms-all-fields\/#1-toc-title\">11 form fields<\/a> which are enough to create any simple forms for your website. Whereas the <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/homepage\/additional-premium-fields\/\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">premium form fields<\/a> allow creating more advanced forms.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, let\u2019s take a look at other features of the Everest Forms form builder.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"editable-form-name\">Editable Form Name<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can change the form name later in the form builder. You can check out the <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs-category\/individual-form-settings\/\" data-type=\"link\" data-id=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs-category\/individual-form-settings\/\">documentation<\/a> for this tab.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Integrations:<\/strong> Only premium users will be able to view and access this tab as it contains the settings for the integrations through the Everest Forms add-ons.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Payments:<\/strong> Same as Integrations, only the users with the pro plan with PayPal and Stripe Add-on will be able to access this tab.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Field Options:<\/strong> This tab will show the field settings such as labels, descriptions, required options, and others for each form field. Clicking on the form field will display the Field Options automatically.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Shortcode:<\/strong> This field provides you with the shortcode for the individual form. This shortcode and be used to insert your form to any post or pages.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Preview:<\/strong> Clicking on the preview button will give a live preview of what your form looks like currently in the frontend of your website.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"row-settings-format\">Row Settings\/Format:<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can select each row&#8217;s format to be one, two, three, or four columns. For this, hover to the respective row, and on the top right, click on the &#8216;pencil&#8217; icon.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"708\" height=\"360\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/10\/Edit-Rows.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3321\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/10\/Edit-Rows.png 708w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/10\/Edit-Rows-300x153.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/10\/Edit-Rows-360x183.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 708px) 100vw, 708px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, select the desired number of columns for the row.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Lastly, you can click on the <strong>SAVE<\/strong> button next to the PREVIEW option to save your form. Now, your form will be added to the All Forms tab of Everest Forms. To easily install Everest Forms, follow the simple steps below:<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"How to Install the Free Version of Everest Forms?\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/UyXA_52Dbj4?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installing Plugin Through Dashboard<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Login to your WordPress website and go to your <strong>Dashboard.<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Find the option for <strong>Plugins<\/strong> and simply click on <strong>Add New.<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>On the Search Bar, type in<strong> Everest Forms<\/strong> and click on Search.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once you find it, Click<strong> Install.<\/strong> After completing the installation click <strong>Activate<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Additionally, if you are using FTP to transfer your files to your server.
